### 4.12 M2.4 UI projection — second sub-slice: search enrichment + summary display + `.md` download + coverage line (owner GO 2026-09-07)

**Sub-slice boundary (owner GO 2026-09-07, charter locked at first-sub-slice closure):** of the §3 M2.4 charter, THIS sub-slice ships exactly the four remaining items — **search enrichment** (§7's second code path), the **per-repo classification `summary` display + its search-corpus entry** (the ss1 carried payload lands), the **same-origin `.md` download**, and the **3-number coverage line** (§5) discharging the F2 re-entry pin. **No new canonical state fields, no codec/emit/reset change, no loader change** (M2.3 surfaces stay closed; the loader already exposes everything this sub-slice consumes: `coverage`, `summary` on the join records, the taxonomy). **Orientation-only, deliberately NOT absorbed (owner directive):** F4 (AI readiness-coherence parity — independent debt, dispositions unchanged), F15 (mount-time fetch sequencing — re-examined this orientation: the hook's mount-anchored lifecycle carries its F3 rationale inline, production never replaces the loader, no concrete correctness consequence found; stays deferred), R8-B (discovery hardening) and R12-5 (AI canonical-form parity) untouched. Group-by-primary stays "later" (§3).

**Search enrichment (§7 discharged — SEQ-1 flipped from absence to presence contract):**

- Corpus when the layer is coherent-ready = base repo fields + AI parts (existing) + the repo's classification **taxonomy labels** (primary + each secondary, resolved by id via the loaded taxonomy) + the record **`summary`**. Category id **slugs deliberately do NOT enter the corpus** — they are URL-canonical machine tokens, not search vocabulary; SEQ-1 keeps its ss1 id-exclusion arm so the flip cannot silently widen into "ids too".
- Mechanism: `buildSearchText(repo, skillCategoryLabels?)` appends the skills parts only when `repo.skills` is present; `prepareRepositories` gains the label-map parameter and its memo key. Readiness gating is **structural and inherited**, not re-decided: RepositoryView passes the join map AND the label map only under the ss1 coherent-ready gate (status `ready` AND data), so a not-ready layer cannot reach the corpus — `repo.skills` is null everywhere (the M24-BDG-1 gate now also guards search).
- Degraded semantics (§7, both directions pinned): not-ready ⇒ corpus = base fields only, and a query only a classification label/summary would match **legitimately yields 0 — never "show all"** (the fail-open mirror: enrichment absence must narrow honestly, never widen).

**Summary display (SEQ-2 flipped from absence to presence contract):** ready + classified ⇒ the card renders the record `summary` as a dedicated line (`.card-skill-summary`, muted, after the GitHub description and before the AI section — curated metadata reads above the AI layer in the trust order); not-ready or unclassified ⇒ absent, structurally (`repo.skills` null — the same gate as badges). SKILLS-3's unclassified-card byte-equality is untouched by construction.

**`.md` download (§1's same-origin download discharged):** ready ⇒ the Skills-ecosystem section carries a same-origin download link (`{BASE_URL}skills-classified.md`, `download` attribute, visible link text naming the format); hidden with the section otherwise (no probe, no runtime state — the link's target is deploy-guaranteed, below). Deploy: a new **`stageSkillsSourceDocument`** stage publishes the vendored `.md` into dist **only after verifying `sha256(md bytes) === source_sha256` of the meta actually present in dist** (coherence with the SERVED artifact, not the working tree — a root `.md` edited without regeneration must never be served as the provenance of an artifact it did not produce). _(Pre-commit R1 amendments — three-leg convergent findings, each reproduced:)_ the stage **serializes with pair staging under the same `skills-classification.json.stage-lock`** (exclusive-create; held-lock or unacquirable-lock ⇒ named fail-soft skip), closing the read-meta→publish race a concurrent pair re-stage could exploit; **every under-lock outcome that is not a successful publish converges the destination** (R2b sol widened this from "every named skip" — the publish-FAILURE arm and inner hard failures owe the same duty, or an ENOSPC mid-write beside a stale pre-existing copy ships those bytes next to a fresh meta): a pre-existing regular-file `dist/skills-classified.md` is RETAINED only when its bytes hash-match the served meta (a still-coherent earlier stage; the read sha is hoisted so even a hard failure after the meta read keeps retain-if-coherent) and otherwise REMOVED so the download honestly degrades to 404 instead of serving bytes that are not the served artifact's source (a failed removal of adjudicated-stale bytes ⇒ `residue: true`, deploy fails per the round-11 contract); a DIRECTORY at the destination is an obstruction — refused and left untouched, no residue (the pair protocol's R15 obstruction precedent; directory removal was never in contract). Every post-open publish failure (write, close, rename — each drivable via the `writeImpl`/`closeImpl`/`openImpl` seams, the M2.2 io-seam precedent) funnels through one cleanup path that removes this run's temp and reports `residue: true` only when that removal fails, and the stuck-lock `warning` survives every path because the generic catch lives INSIDE the lock scope. Absent dist meta / unparsable dist meta / absent source / hash mismatch ⇒ **named fail-soft skip** (the deploy proceeds). Destination overwrite by the final rename is the intended publish operation (the same convergence the pair protocol performs at its canonical names); the never-overwrite-foreign-files discipline applies to the unique TEMP name, which is exclusive-created. Runs in the deploy CLI `stage` command after `stageSkillsArtifacts`, reported through the same one-string pure-formatter pattern (`formatSkillsSourceStageReport`, warning line included — a stuck lock is surfaced, mirroring the pair's `warning` semantics); a skip never blocks the canonical deploy; rejected bytes are never written at all — verification precedes the first write.

**Coverage line (§5's three-numbers rule discharged + F2 re-entry pin):** ready ⇒ the Skills-ecosystem section renders the three **generation-time** numbers from loader `coverage`, **separately labeled, never summed, never conflated into a readiness signal, never live-recomputed** (§4.4 forbids live re-check): `N matched · N unclassified · N unresolved source entries`, prefixed as generation-time coverage. **F2 re-entry pin discharged as stated by the owner ruling:** ready + `coverage.matched === 0` ⇒ the layer **stays READY** (facet section renders, scope/category filtering semantics unchanged — filtering keys on the join map, never on `coverage`), and the coverage UI carries an explicit zero-coverage sentence. `matched = 0` affects presentation only: it never derives `unavailable`, never hides the section, never suppresses or neutralizes a filter.

**Acceptance criteria (each pinned by a named test; SEQ-1/SEQ-2 keep their ss1 names — flipped, not replaced, so the lineage from absence pin to presence contract stays visible):**

| ID         | Test                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| SEQ-1      | ready join: a taxonomy-label-only term and a summary-only term both match the classified repo's `searchText`; the id slug does NOT; base fields still match; without the layer the corpus is base-only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| SEQ-2      | ready + classified ⇒ the card renders the record summary (inside the card subtree); ready + unclassified ⇒ absent; not-ready (data present, status loading) ⇒ absent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| M24-SRCH-1 | integration (full view): ready + label-only query narrows to exactly the classified repos; unavailable + the same query ⇒ 0 results with NoResults — never "show all"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| M24-COV-1  | ready ⇒ coverage line shows the three loader numbers separately labeled in the Skills-ecosystem section; not-ready ⇒ absent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| M24-COV-2  | ready + `matched: 0` ⇒ section still renders (READY preserved) + explicit zero-coverage sentence + scope/facet still filter by the join map (a non-empty map still narrows) + no degraded notice + the download link AND the category facet stay present (R1 L2 — a matched-gated link/facet mutant must redden)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| M24-DL-1   | ready ⇒ same-origin `{BASE_URL}skills-classified.md` link with `download` attribute in the Skills-ecosystem section; not-ready ⇒ absent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| DEPLOY-MD  | staged when `sha256(md) === dist meta.source_sha256` (bytes re-hashed, not trusted); named skip on hash mismatch / absent source / absent-or-invalid dist meta / held lock; every skip converges the destination (retain iff it hash-matches the served meta, else removed; failed stale-removal ⇒ `residue`); post-open publish failures funnel to temp cleanup with `residue` on failed cleanup (injected-rm pin); lock released after the run; a skip never blocks the canonical deploy — pinned at CLI level too (fixture dataDir with no skills files ⇒ exit 0 + named skip lines) |
| M24-DL-2   | non-root `BASE_URL` (stubbed) ⇒ href = `{base}skills-classified.md` — a hardcoded-root href mutant must redden (R1 sol; tests otherwise run at `/` and cannot see the Pages subpath)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

With that one item fixed, every luna@max finding across the arc is dispositioned. luna@ultra: FOURTH consecutive quota interruption (216k tokens; next reset 17:36) — that single lens keeps its recorded gap.** **R3c (owner ruling: luna@ultra excluded from the lineup; focused closure on the corrected record, 2026-09-08 17:38, fingerprint-guarded): luna@max = PROCEED — "The sole FIX is correctly resolved: S1/S3/S4/S5 are distinguished as fixed, while S2 remains explicitly accepted as a bounded residual. No production or test delta requires reopening." With luna@ultra excluded by owner ruling, every lineup lens ends at a literal PROCEED on this tree: sol@max (R3) + luna@max (R3c), the 13 non-spec files byte-identical across both reviews (joint per-file diff hash `5ca1cc7efe8f8e13` proven both sides). ### 4.13 M2.5 integrated closure — verification-first, production delta 0 (owner GO 2026-09-09)

**Slice contract (owner GO 2026-09-09, `feat/m2.5-classification-closure` cut from `origin/main@675bb3f`).** M2.5 is the M1.2f-pattern **verification-only closure** of the classification layer: its job is to prove that M2.1–M2.4, _as one integrated layer_, actually closes — NOT to add feature surface. **Default contract: verification-first, `production delta = 0`** — zero change to app / production / deploy source; the deliverable is integrated tests + this section. No new canonical state field, codec/emit/reset change, loader, generator, or UI feature is in scope. Group-by-primary view mode stays "later" (§3, §8). The four deferred debts and the accepted residuals are carried, NOT absorbed (rules below). One targeted browser smoke on the built app closes the observable-UI surface M2.4 shipped. Orientation done at kickoff (2026-09-09): `origin/main` unchanged at `675bb3f` since the #271 merge (no post-merge automation drift into any classification/spec/dashboard surface), and a semantic status-sweep of every deferred debt / accepted residual confirmed none is mis-stated as fixed.

**The M2 integrated invariant matrix (what M2.5 proves as a whole).** Load-bearing invariants spanning the layer, each already sectioned; M2.5 verifies they hold _together_, not just per-slice:

- **Identity / join (§4.1, §5):** runtime join is `node_id → live repo` only; record identity and the `.md`'s name/stars/url/language are never trusted at runtime; an unclassified live repo is an ordinary Starred repo; a classification whose `node_id` misses the live dataset simply does not surface (layer stays `ready`).
- **Availability model (§2.2, §4.5, §4.10 decision 1):** `loading | ready | unavailable`; `loading ≠ unavailable`; validation never fail-open (whole-artifact accept/reject, no partial salvage).
- **Coherent-ready gate (§4.11/§4.12):** `skillsReady = status === 'ready' && data != null` — BOTH halves required; neither status-alone nor data-alone activates; both incoherent directions fail closed toward the base browser. This one boolean gates joins, filtering, facets, badges, summary, search enrichment, coverage, and notices.
- **Fail-soft contract (§2.2):** optional-layer absence never suppresses base repos, never zeroes results, never "shows all silently"; the dependent facet goes inactive (not "results happen to be all"), the degraded state is surfaced, the URL is retained, and suppressed values do not count as effective filters.
- **Filter vs. search-enrichment are distinct code paths (§7):** a filter on missing metadata goes inactive; search enrichment simply narrows the corpus to base fields — a label-only query then legitimately yields 0, never "show all".
- **Integrity / byte contract (§4.10):** digest of received bytes, decode only after match, across all four loaders; build⇔runtime byte AND decode (BOM) agreement; no integrity opt-out surface.
- **Coverage semantics (§4.4, §5, §4.12):** three generation-time numbers, separately labeled, never summed, never conflated into a readiness signal, never live-recomputed; `matched = 0` is presentation-only and never derives `unavailable`.

**Integrated acceptance matrix (each row pinned by a named integrated test established in the M2.5 record; a row that reproduces existing-behavior RED is a newly-discovered blocker — policy below, not a silent in-slice fix).** Formalizes the owner's A–J; the value is one integrated exercise proving several invariants at once, not a re-run of the per-slice unit pins:

| ID        | Integrated claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| M25-A     | **Identity/join:** classification attaches only by `node_id`; no wrong-repo attachment; an unclassified repo renders as a normal Starred repo (card byte-equivalent to a layer-absent render, the SKILLS-3 property, at integration scope)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| M25-B     | **Runtime state:** `loading`, `ready`+data, and `unavailable` each behave per contract; every incoherent `status`×`data` combination fails soft to the base browser (the coherent-ready gate, exercised at integration scope)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| M25-C     | **Scope + facet:** `scope=skills` and `skill=<category>` narrow when `ready`; the requested URL state survives not-`ready` (retained, inactive, uncounted); suppressed facets change neither results nor the effective-filter count; `ready` activates them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| M25-D     | **Search:** classification labels + `summary` enrich search only when coherent-`ready`; `loading`/`unavailable` cannot leak any classification text (labels, summary) into the corpus — a label-only query then yields 0, never "show all"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| M25-E     | **Cards:** badges appear only with a coherent-`ready` classification; an unclassified card stays visually equivalent to a base card; long real taxonomy labels (up to the §4.2 120-char bound) stay contained at the narrowest column and ≤320px                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| M25-F     | **Summary:** the curated `summary` line renders only where the ss2 presence contract allows (`ready` + classified); no stale summary survives when the layer is not `ready`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| M25-G     | **Coverage:** `matched / unclassified / unresolved` are internally coherent and separately labeled; `matched = 0` keeps the layer READY (never → `unavailable`) and communicates zero coverage explicitly; filtering still keys on the join map, never on `coverage`, and the non-empty join map also drives taxonomy-LABEL search enrichment while `matched = 0`                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| M25-H     | **Download:** the `.md` link appears only with a coherent-`ready` layer and disappears when not-`ready` (the structural `a[href$="skills-classified.md"]` oracle: M25-A present / fail-soft absent); a not-`ready`/failure never damages the base browser. Deploy-time byte-correspondence (served `.md` sha == served meta `source_sha256`) is the `DEPLOY-MD` unit pin + the browser smoke — not a jsdom oracle                                                                                                                                                                |
| M25-I     | **Canonical URL interaction:** `scope`/`skill` round-trip is shown composed in M25-J; §6.2 out-of-range reconciliation via `replace` is the M25-RECON pin; clear-all clears the scope+skill query axis while preserving `density` is the M25-I integrated pin; per-field codec round-trip + page-reset-on-semantic-change stay the URL-1 / RST-1 unit pins (cited, not duplicated)                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| M25-J     | **Cross-feature transition (flagship):** coherent-`ready` + a skill facet + classification-enriched search compose as a DISCRIMINATING INTERSECTION (dropping EITHER dimension changes the asserted result set), with the winner's badge + curated `summary`, the canonical URL for that transition, and `density` surviving the keystroke. (Out-of-range `page` clamp + `replace` reconciliation is **M25-RECON**; the coverage line is **M25-G** + M24-COV-1; `view` is untouched by a partial query update and is deliberately NOT claimed here — its fail-soft is §6.4/M1.1) |
| M25-RECON | **§6.2 page reconciliation (composed):** a bookmarked out-of-range `page` over a ≥2-page filtered result set clamps to the effective last page, and the canonical URL is REWRITTEN to it via `replace` — no history entry added (`history.length` unchanged); a reconciliation no-op (URL stays out-of-range) or a `push` (history grows) reddens                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

**Fail-soft closure matrix — composed two-layer evidence (owner ruling 2026-09-09).** M25-FS-1…7 together provide closure evidence through a composed two-layer matrix, NOT seven independent raw-fetch-to-DOM chains: the canonical loader/hook tests prove the **failure → state mapping**, while the M2.5 integration tests here prove the **fail-soft projection conjunction**. Representative **artifact-missing** and **integrity-failure** cases additionally traverse the real loader-to-projection chain end-to-end.

- **Layer 1 — failure→state mapping (canonical, cited not rebuilt):** the existing `load-skills-classification.test.ts` / `use-skills-classification.test.tsx` suite is the terminal proof that `artifact missing / artifact malformed / meta malformed / integrity failure / unsupported version / load rejection ⇒ unavailable`. M2.5 confirms those tests still terminal-cover the mapping; it does NOT duplicate them for count.
- **Layer 2 — projection conjunction (M25-FS-\*, `renderView` level):** for the layer `unavailable` (and, in flight, `loading`), every row proves the SAME conjunction — base Starred repos fully usable; search / sort / pagination / density operate on the un-narrowed base set; every classification-dependent behavior (scope, facet, badges, summary, search enrichment, coverage, download) neutralized; the requested recoverable URL state (scope/skill values, chips) retained.
- **End-to-end representative chains (real loader → real projection):** two composed tests close the composition boundary the two layers otherwise only meet at a seam.

| ID           | Level                              | Claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------ | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| M25-FS-1…6   | Layer 1 (cited)                    | the canonical loader/hook tests map each failure condition (artifact missing/malformed, meta malformed, integrity failure, unsupported version, load rejection) to `unavailable` — cited and confirmed terminal-complete, not rebuilt                                    |
| M25-FS-proj  | Layer 2 (`renderView`)             | layer `unavailable`/`loading` ⇒ the full fail-soft projection conjunction above (base usable, every classification-dependent behavior neutralized, requested URL state retained, base search/sort/page/density functional)                                               |
| M25-FS-7     | projection (coherent-ready mirror) | the `status`×`data` incoherence contract, BOTH directions, pinned directly (NOT a loader-failure taxonomy member): `status=ready` + `data=null` ⇒ inactive (no match-nothing filter, no silent zero-results); `status≠ready` + `data≠null` ⇒ inactive                    |
| M25-FS-E2E-1 | real loader → projection           | **artifact missing:** stubbed fetch 404 → real `useSkillsClassification` → `unavailable` → real projection conjunction (transport/source-absence path)                                                                                                                   |
| M25-FS-E2E-2 | real loader → projection           | **integrity failure:** valid-looking artifact bytes + a mismatching declared `classification_sha256` → real loader fails closed BEFORE decode/projection → `unavailable` → real projection conjunction (the M2.3 load-bearing "data arrived, must fail closed" boundary) |

**Browser smoke (final, on the built app + real dataset, per the M2.4 precedent; manual, disclosed in the record).** READY — scope / facet / badges / summary / coverage line / `.md` download all reachable and correct. LOADING / UNAVAILABLE — no classification projection leaks (no badges, summary, coverage, or enriched search hits), notices + chips usable, base browser intact. `matched = 0` — layer stays READY, coverage states zero explicitly, no false degraded state. Desktop + narrow (≤320px) — no document overflow, sidebar/drawer parity.

**Deferred debts & accepted residuals — carried, explicitly NOT absorbed.** M2.5 does not touch, fix, or fold in any of the following; a reviewer MAY challenge a containment, but **only NEW evidence disproving the recorded containment reopens it — merely re-describing the same corner does not.**

- **Deferred debts (independent, owned elsewhere):** F4 (AI readiness-coherence parity — §4.11), F15 (classification mount-fetch sequencing — §4.11/§4.12, re-examined twice: no concrete correctness consequence, stays deferred), R8-B (discovery `node_id` uniqueness / `source_count` parity — §4.10), R12-5 (AI canonical-form parity — §4.10 Ruling 2).
- **Accepted bounded residuals (living in main, owner-adjudicated):** last-write-wins optional-pair concurrency (§4.10 Ruling 1), crash-between-two-renames torn pair (§4.10 R15 — flagged as `residue` ⇒ deploy fails, never a silent torn upload), S2 pair-local stuck-lock double-failure on a reused dist (§4.12 — non-CI, doubly log-visible).

**Newly-discovered-blocker policy (owner ruling 2026-09-09 — Option 1, verbatim).** If integrated verification reproduces a real correctness defect that blocks M2 closure, exists in already-merged M2.1–M2.4 behavior, is not an already-accepted residual, and is not an already-deferred debt:

```text
1. Reproduce first against the current frozen tree.
2. Establish a discriminating RED reproducer/test or executable probe.
3. Classify the violated normative invariant and consequence.
4. Propose the narrowest credible remediation.
5. STOP before making any production change.
6. Return to owner for disposition.

Until owner ruling:
- do not patch production;
- do not silently spin it out as deferred;
- do not declare M2.5 complete;
- preserve production delta = 0.
```

**RED-first boundary (owner, 2026-09-09).** Before stopping back to the owner, the reproduction may use a disposable probe, an isolated-worktree mutation, a temporary failing test, source/destination observation, and a minimal-fix _proposal_ — but a **production fix is never landed in the working tree**, and even an auditable regression reproducer is brought back as a **proposed test diff first**, never permanently mutated into the M2.5 tree ("it's only a test" is not a licence to mutate early). This lets the owner rule in one pass on: finding validity, whether the test oracle is faithful to the contract, and remediation scope. **Status semantics on a reproduced blocker: `M2.5 = PAUSED_ON_REPRODUCED_BLOCKER`** — never `FAILED`, `DEFERRED`, or `COMPLETE-WITH-NOTE`. The owner's disposition may be a narrow `M2.4.x` remediation slice (reproduce → narrow fix → merge → fresh main → re-run the affected integrated matrix → resume M2.5 closure) — but that spin-out is an owner ruling, never an agent default.

**Review strategy (full milestone-closure final gate, not a light per-slice gate).** implementation/integration tests → local gates (`vitest` · typecheck · eslint · prettier · build) → browser smoke → mutation / falsification for the load-bearing integrated claims (predictions written before each run; every load-bearing pin proven able to fail for the intended reason) → fresh cross-model review → amendment only on a reproduced real finding → final frozen-head cross-family gate on the exact SHA → **M2 CLOSED**. The ideal terminal state: the integrated matrix all green with `production delta = 0`. A non-zero production delta appears only via a ratified blocker disposition (policy above), never to make M2.5 "look implemented".

**Load-bearing falsification set (owner ruling 2026-09-09 — pin boundaries, not a mechanical one-mutant-per-test symmetry).** The mutation/falsification effort targets exactly these load-bearing boundaries, each proven able to go RED for the intended reason (prediction written first): (1) **E2E artifact-missing** — a missing artifact that incorrectly yields `READY` must RED (M25-FS-E2E-1); (2) **E2E integrity** — a digest mismatch bypassed, or decode continuing past a failed integrity check, must RED (M25-FS-E2E-2); (3) **coherent-ready / status** — `skillsReady` ignoring `status` (activating on data alone) must RED; (4) **coherent-ready / data** — `skillsReady` ignoring data coherence (activating on `status` alone) must RED; (5) **fail-soft neutralization** — classification-dependent filtering/search still active while `unavailable` must RED; (6) **requested-state retention** — degraded reconciliation deleting recoverable `scope`/`skill` URL state must RED. Loader failure classes already carry existing discriminating tests: M2.5 cites them and confirms they stay terminal-complete rather than duplicating a mutant per class for count.

**M2.5 = M2 closure definition.** Closing M2.5 closes M2 (the classification layer, M2.1–M2.5); M3 (identity — header ledger mark + `/favicon.svg`, §9) is the remaining milestone. The deferred debts (F4 / F15 / R8-B / R12-5) remain recorded-open after M2 closes — they are NOT re-homed into M2.5 and NOT a condition of M2 closure; they carry forward as independent debt.

Orientation verdict at kickoff: no post-#271 automation drift (`origin/main` still `675bb3f`), and a semantic sweep found no deferred debt / accepted residual mis-stated as fixed. The suite proves the cross-feature compositions, not the per-slice unit pins (cited, not duplicated): **M25-A** node_id-only join (own-badges/summary, unclassified renders plain, a ghost record is a non-event, layer stays ready); **M25-B** a not-ready bookmarked scope/skill re-applies once the layer settles; **M25-J** (flagship) one transition — ready + skill filter + enriched search compose as a discriminating INTERSECTION ⇒ correct filtered+searched set, the winner's badges + curated summary, the transition's canonical URL, density survival (co-covers C/D/E/F + the scope/skill URL round-trip); the out-of-range `page` clamp + `replace` reconciliation is **M25-RECON** (over a ≥2-page FILTERED subset so the clamp is shown to key on the filtered result count, not the raw repo count) and clear-all is **M25-I**; **M25-G** `matched=0` stays READY with the non-empty join map still filtering; **M25-FS-proj** the full fail-soft conjunction under `unavailable` and `loading` (base usable, everything classification-dependent inert, requested URL retained, base search/sort/density functional, enrichment-only query honestly 0 — never "show all"); **M25-FS-7** the coherent-ready gate's BOTH incoherent directions; **M25-FS-E2E-1/2** the two real loader→projection chains (a stubbed 404, and valid bytes + a mismatching declared digest → the loader fails closed before decode → the projection degrades). Composed FS evidence per the owner ruling: Layer-1 failure→state mapping stays the existing `load-skills-classification.test.ts` (MATRIX-2 artifact-missing, MATRIX-3 meta-missing, MATRIX-4/MATRIX-5 malformed, MATRIX-6 unsupported-version, MATRIX-7 network, + the integrity byte-hash-mismatch case) and `use-skills-classification.test.tsx` (null→unavailable), cited and confirmed terminal-complete; Layer-2 conjunction + the two E2E are the new integration tests. **No blocker reproduced — every integrated assertion is GREEN on the current tree**, so M2.1–M2.4 compose as one layer without a new closure-blocking defect. Gates: dashboard `vitest run` **286/286** (22 files, +11), workspace `pnpm -r test` all green, `pnpm -r typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `prettier --check` clean. **Load-bearing falsification set — 5 production mutants, each RED-first for the intended reason then reverted byte-clean** (`git checkout --`, working tree re-verified): `skillsReady` dropping the status half → M25-B/FS-proj(loading)/FS-7 red; dropping the data half → FS-7's match-nothing arm red; `dashboardToView` ignoring `skillsReady` for `skillsScope` → every not-ready test red (incl. both E2E, which thereby also discriminate the neutralization); a "tidy the stale facet on degrade" clear of scope/skill → the URL-retention assertions red; the loader bypassing `readBytesVerified` for a plain `.text()` decode → M25-FS-E2E-2 red. ## 5. Join & drift rules (verified 2026-08-10)

Observed against a **697-repo `stars.json`** — per git history that count corresponds to the **2026-07-30 dataset snapshot (`55ae7b2`)**, the state the 169/171 analysis ran against; by the spec's own landing commit (`2987e92`, 2026-08-10) the daily-synced dataset was already 734, and 742 at M1.2's close. These coverage figures are historical to the `55ae7b2` snapshot; M2 recomputes them at generation time: **169/171 exact-name matches**; the 2 unresolved are `jacob-bd/notebooklm-mcp-cli` and `AgentWrapper/agent-orchestrator` (both absent — `grep` count 0). Stars at that snapshot contain similarly-named `jacob-bd/gemini-notebook-mcp-cli` and `Untrivial-ai/agent-orchestrator` — **plausible renames, NOT proof of identity.** So `697 − 169 = 528` unclassified at that snapshot.

- The join is **build-time** (`name_with_owner → node_id`); runtime joins `node_id → live repo` only, and **never** trusts the `.md`'s name/stars/url/language.
- **No fuzzy remapping, ever.** The only admissible resolution sources: (1) case-insensitive exact `name_with_owner` match against the generation-snapshot stars; (2) a **human-reviewed durable alias map** (`old_owner/old_repo → node_id`, shape in §4.7), added only after manual confirmation; (3) a prior generated record's stored `node_id`. Otherwise the entry stays `unresolved` and is retained + surfaced.
- **Executable resolution rule (M2.1 — evaluate-all, not first-hit).** For EVERY entry the generator evaluates all three sources (each lookup case-insensitive on `source_name_with_owner`; a prior record with `node_id: null` contributes no candidate) and collects the distinct non-null candidate `node_id`s: **zero** → `unresolved` (retained, `resolution: "missing_from_stars"`); **exactly one** → resolved to it; **more than one** → **build FAILURE with the entry and every candidate id named**. A first-hit/short-circuit walk is explicitly wrong — it cannot even see the exact-vs-alias conflict (a recycled name: alias says A, a live exact match now says B) or the exact-vs-prior conflict (same-name different-repo swap) that this rule exists to surface. Silently preferring any source would paper over exactly that drift; the fix is human (update the `.md` or the alias map).
- **Auxiliary-input edge semantics (M2.1):** a **stale alias row** (its `source_name_with_owner` matches no current `.md` entry) is NOT a build failure — it resolves nothing, so it can corrupt nothing; the generator surfaces it in diagnostics so the map gets pruned (requiring atomic `.md`+map edits for every entry removal would be friction without integrity gain). A **present-but-invalid prior artifact** is a build failure with a named escape (§4.6). Alias/prior lineage is fingerprinted in meta (§4.4).
- **`node_id` is stable across renames** — so once the 2 missing ids are recovered into the source (one-time, human-confirmed), a later owner rename resolves automatically to the repo's new name.
- **Three distinct coverage numbers** (never conflate): `169 matched · 528 unclassified · 2 unresolved source entries`. The downloadable metadata / coverage diagnostics MUST retain the unresolved names + reasons — satisfied by the artifact itself (§4.2: the retained `source_name_with_owner` + `resolution` as the machine reason) plus the alias map's human `reason` for recovered ids; no separate per-entry note field exists.

---

## 6. Canonical URL-state contract (M1)

One canonical state → URL projection. Params conceptually: `view`, `scope`, `skill` (category), `q`, filters, `sort`, `direction`, `density`, `page`, later `group`. **M1.1** lands the mechanics for the fields that exist today — `view`, `density`, `page` plus the M0 sort/direction codec — and the reconciliation model; `scope`/`skill` arrived with M2.4 (§4.11); `group` arrives with M4.1 (§15.3, §6.5).

- Unspecified → default; **defaults are omitted from the URL** (deterministic canonical serialization; equivalent states serialize byte-identically — the existing P1 property). The default state serializes to `''`.
- Invalid enum → default. Malformed URL never crashes the app.
- Canonical emit order is fixed: `view`, then the existing P1 order (`q`, `sort`, `direction`, array facets, booleans, release/hydration), then `density`, then `page`. _(M2.4 amendment: `scope`, then `skill`, slot directly after `view` — §4.11.)_ _(M4.1 amendment: `group` slots directly after `skill`, before `q` — the skills cluster stays contiguous; §15.3.)_
- Back/Forward fully restores state (existing `popstate` path).

### 6.1 Sort/direction codec — R1 convergence (M1.1)

- **Decode:** `direction = <valid direction param> ?? defaultDirection(sort)` — a _missing_ `direction` resolves to the sort field's natural default (M0's `defaultDirection`), **not** a global `desc`. This is the R1 fix (previously M1-deferred).
- **Encode:** emit `sort` iff it differs from the default sort; emit `direction` iff `direction !== defaultDirection(sort)`. The two params are now **independent** — a non-default sort at its natural direction emits `sort` alone (this supersedes the P1 "sort+direction always travel together" rule; test `URL-5` is updated with rationale).
- **Backward-compat:** a legacy URL carrying a _redundant_ default `direction` (e.g. `sort=name_with_owner&direction=asc`) still decodes correctly; the redundancy is simply dropped on the next canonical re-serialization.

### 6.2 Page — requested vs. effective (M1.1)

- **Decode accepts the requested page** (`page >= 1`; `< 1`, non-integer, or absent → `1`). It is **not** clamped against the dataset at decode time — `normalizeDashboardState` has no result count and stays pure.
- **Effective page** is derived where the filtered result count is known (`RepositoryView`): `lastPage = max(1, ceil(resultCount / 48))`; `effectivePage = clamp(requestedPage, 1, lastPage)`. Rendering slices by `effectivePage`.
- **Reconciliation:** when `requestedPage !== effectivePage`, the canonical URL is **`replace`-written** with `effectivePage` (no history push). This is how "serialize writes the effective/canonical value" is realized without a data-aware codec, and it converges in one step (the rewritten `effectivePage` is a fixed point).

### 6.3 Page-reset semantics (M1.1)

- **Reset `page → 1`** on a **semantic value change** to query/search/filter/sort/**view** (`scope` joined this set in M2.4, together with `skillCategories` — §4.11; `group` joins in M4.1 — §15.3) — compare normalized before/after values; a no-op write (re-setting a field to its current value) does **not** reset. `density` change does **not** reset page.
- An **explicit `page`** supplied in the same update **wins** and is never overwritten by the implicit reset. (Rationale: key-presence resets fire on no-op writes and on future refactors that pass through unchanged fields.)

### 6.4 View — capability fail-soft (M1.1)

- `view` (active tab: `stars` | `discovery` | `insights`) is **canonical state**, consolidated out of `App.tsx`'s local `activeView` so there is a single source of truth; the state hook is lifted to `App` and its controls are passed down (no second hook instance writing the URL).
- **Asymmetry with `page` (deliberate):** a bookmarked `view=discovery` while the optional discovery substrate is unavailable is a _recoverable_ request — the **requested value is retained in the URL** and the **effective render falls back to `stars`** (mirrors the M0 AI-facet fail-soft, §2.2), re-applying once discovery loads. Unlike `page`, it is **not** reconciled/rewritten, because it may become valid later; an out-of-range `page` never becomes valid for the current dataset, so it is canonicalized.
- **Empty stars must not dead-end any view:** when discovery is available the tabs remain and a bookmarked `view=discovery` reaches the inbox even with zero stars (the stars pane shows the empty state in place). **Amended in M4.3a (§17.2):** the full-screen `EmptyState` early return this once used is **removed entirely** — the view-tabs nav is always present once stars load, and `EmptyState` renders IN-PANE below the tabs, so no view (`stars` / `discovery` / `insights`) can dead-end on it and Insights stays reachable even from an empty account.
- **`insights` is a THIRD, always-available view (§17, M4.3a — the minimal §6 amendment):** unlike `discovery` it NEVER falls back — it is a deterministic read-only view of the SAME loaded stars dataset, so it is effective whenever stars load. The view-tabs nav is therefore **always present** (`Starred` + `Insights`, plus `Discovery Inbox` only when available), not only when discovery is. While `insights` is active the repo browse state (query / filters / sort / group / scope) is **preserved in the URL but INACTIVE** — RepositoryView is not mounted, so nothing reads or mutates those facets, and they re-apply on return to `stars`; `page` follows the existing §6.3 view-switch reset (a `view` change resets it). No new facet/filter/canonical field is added — only the `view` enum gains `insights`.

### 6.5 Group — presentation fail-soft (M4.1)

- `group` (`none` | `skill`, default `none`) is **canonical presentation state**, in the same class as `view`/`density`: preserved by clear-all, never a chip, never counted as a filter, and it **never enters the selector** (`dashboardToView` / `FilterState`) — grouping is a projection applied after search → filter → sort on the unchanged effective result set (§15.4).
- **Requested vs effective — the §6.4 asymmetry, not the §6.2 one:** a requested `group=skill` while the skills layer is not coherent-ready renders the flat list (base repos never suppressed, §2.2), surfaces a degraded notice, and **retains** the URL value — it is not reconciled/rewritten, because it becomes valid once the layer loads. `page` remains the only reconciled field: when grouped mode is effective `lastPage = 1`, so a requested `page > 1` converges to `1` through the existing §6.2 `replace` rewrite (§15.5).

---

## 7. Filter semantics vs. search-enrichment semantics (distinct — do not share one `hasMetadata ? … : true`)

- **Filter** depending on unavailable metadata → the filter becomes **inactive**, base entities preserved, degraded surfaced (§2.2).
- **Search enrichment**: when the layer is `ready`, the search corpus = repo fields + classification labels; when unavailable, corpus = repo fields only. A query that only a classification label would have matched then legitimately returns 0 — an acceptable **degraded** result, and explicitly **not** "show all". These are different code paths.

---

## 8. Multi-fit presentation (M2)

One repo → one card, never duplicated. Model: `primary_category` + `categories[]`. Badges: `[Security] [+2]` (or full badges when space allows). Group-by-primary view mode uses **only** `primary_category`, so each repo appears exactly once.

`primary_category` must be **deterministic and sourced from the `.md`'s primary/secondary semantics** — the generator must not runtime-pick the first alphabetical category. Encoded explicitly in the generator spec.

---

This section is historical — see `docs/brand-tokens.md` and `docs/adr/ADR-005-portfolio-visual-alignment.md`.

Inline SVG, `currentColor` + `.brand-mark { color: var(--accent) }`, wordmark `--fg`. Favicon: a `/favicon.svg` asset (owner preference over a `data:` URI; both are CSP-legal under `img-src 'self' data:`).

## 12. M1.1 acceptance & review outcome

Implemented on `feat/m1.1-state-contract` (base = M0 `2987e92`).

### Acceptance (all green)

- **Codec** — `view`/`density`/`page` round-trip; defaults omitted; canonical emit order fixed; `normalize`/`parse`/`serialize` idempotent and closed (incl. huge/`Infinity` pages → `MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`).
- **R1** — decode a missing `direction` → `defaultDirection(sort)`; encode omits `direction` when it equals the field default; a legacy redundant `direction` still decodes (dropped on re-serialize). Old test `URL-5`/`M0-SORT-2` updated with rationale.
- **Page** — requested accepted (junk/0/neg/decimal → 1; repeated → last valid); effective clamped in `RepositoryView`; out-of-range reconciled via `replace` (no history push); stale page canonicalized by App when no populated stars surface exists.
- **Reset** — `page → 1` on a semantic change to query/search/filter/sort/view; `density` exempt; no-op update does not reset; explicit `page` wins; clear-all preserves `view` + `density`.
- **View** — consolidated out of `App` local state into canonical `state.view` (single hook lifted to `App`); fail-soft (requested retained, effective falls back to `stars`); empty stars never dead-ends an available discovery view.
- **Pagination proof** — 48/page slicing + minimal Prev/Next; boundary controls use `aria-disabled` (focus-safe) + guarded no-op.

Verified: `vitest` **784/784**, `pnpm -r typecheck`, `eslint .`, `prettier --check` (changed) — all green.

### Cross-model review (Luna@max · Luna@ultra · Sol@max, 3 rounds)

- **R1:** F1 (codec not closed for huge pages), F2 (empty-stars early return hid an available discovery view — Sol + Luna), F3 (pager disabled the focused control). All fixed.
- **R2:** A (clear-all also wiped `view`/`density` — real regression), B (overlong digit page → `Infinity` → 1), C (page not reconciled when `RepositoryView` unmounted). All fixed. D (tab-visibility vs `effectiveView` divergence, Luna-max) dismissed.
- **R3:** Sol-max **PROCEED**, Luna-ultra **PROCEED** (all fixes confirmed; D independently verified non-existent). Luna-max re-raised **D**; **confirmed false positive** — `App.tsx` tab visibility is `discovery && discovery.candidates.length > 0`, byte-identical to `discoveryAvailable`, so an empty-`candidates` payload hides the tab and its failure scenario cannot occur (ground-truth verified).

Outcome: every real finding across three rounds fixed with a pinned regression test; the sole outstanding BLOCK is a verified misread. Ready for owner commit.

## 13. M1.2 acceptance boundary — browser interaction + compact-density UX

M1.1 built the correctness substrate; M1.2 is a pure interaction/presentation slice and does
NOT reopen the canonical-state architecture. Implement in order (each owner-reviewed; xcheck
before each commit). R3 leads because it still carries semantic correctness, so it is not
buried under the later CSS/layout diffs.

**M1.2a — R3 effective-filter-count convergence.** The Filters toggle badge/count reflects the
**effective** (active) filter count. Requested-but-inactive optional-AI filters
(`categories`/`aiTags` while the AI layer is not `ready`) do **not** inflate the badge; they
remain visible as removable chips + a degraded notice (§2.2), consistent with the result
summary's existing `effectiveFilterCount`. Once the AI layer becomes `ready` and the requested
filter activates, the badge updates.

**M1.2b — density control + compact/comfortable presentation.** A control writes ONLY canonical
`density`; `compact` is the §6 default. Density change preserves the current page;
reload/back/forward reproduce the density. `compact` materially raises information density with
no content/action removed to achieve it; `comfortable` stays usable and visually distinct; both
preserve repository actions/links.

**M1.2c — sticky toolbar.** Search/filter/sort/view/density controls stay reachable while
browsing; must not obscure content; narrow-viewport behavior explicitly tested; no second local
owner for canonical controls (an ephemeral `is-scrolled` presentation flag is fine).

**M1.2d — facet-scroll.** Long facet groups get bounded scrolling; the page itself must not
overgrow because one facet is large; keyboard/mouse scrolling stays usable; selected values
remain reachable (the active-filter chips already surface selections).

**M1.2e — topic expand/collapse.** Deterministic collapsed default/threshold; selected topics
never hidden behind a collapsed section without indication. This is **LOCAL presentation state,
NOT canonical URL state** — §6 is not extended for it.

**M1.2f — integrated behavioral/regression pass.** density→URL, density-does-not-reset-page,
sticky presence/continuity, facet overflow, topic collapse, and the R3
loading/unavailable/ready matrix — all green together.

**Round 1 (2026-08-13, Codex xhigh on the seven-commit PR):** three findings, all confirmed by reproduction and accepted. **R1-1 (correctness, M1.2c amendment):** the stuck toolbar (bottom ≈55px, opaque, z 10) permanently covered the sticky sidebar's top 39px (`top: 1rem`) — the Language section header hit-tested to TOOLBAR; missed because jsdom tests pin structure/classes, not geometry, and the slice smoke never probed the sidebar's own sticky offset. Fixed: RepositoryView publishes the layout-dependent toolbar height as `--toolbar-h` (ResizeObserver; jsdom-guarded), the sidebar's `top`/`max-height` consume it (tabs variant mirrored); STICK-4 pins the wiring; browser re-smoke measures the clearance. **R1-2 (correctness, M1.2b amendment):** `.density-compact .card-list` silently defeated the pre-existing ≤900px single-column media rule (equal-or-higher specificity, later in source) — at 800px compact rendered 2 columns while comfortable honored 1; the CSS comment's "never fights the responsive rules" claim was false. Fixed: the column override now lives inside `@media (min-width: 901px)` (spacing stays densified at all widths), comment corrected, browser re-smoke at 800px. **R1-3 (provenance):** the M1.2d record described a `showAll`-keyed intermediate that no committed snapshot carries (working-tree-only between R1 and its fix) — a precision note now states this explicitly in the record. Gates after amendments: `vitest run` **811/811**, typecheck/eslint/prettier ✓; amendment diff reviewed by the standard three-lens lineup before commit (see the round-2 entry below once the Codex loop re-runs).

**Round 2 (2026-08-13, Codex xhigh on the amended PR):** confirmed all three round-1 fixes resolved; **one new finding (Medium, provenance):** the M1.2d record read as chronologically contradictory — "backfill pending until the 2026-08-18 quota reset" followed by "completed 2026-08-13" left an auditor unable to tell early-capacity from a jumped gate. Accepted as a wording-precision defect (the substance was correct: the owner restored capacity same-day, ping-verified, before any leg ran): the pending clause now states the precondition as **restored capacity** (the 2026-08-18 date recorded as the then-stated provider reset, not a hard gate) with an explicit timeline-precision note. No production/test change; doc-only correction per the evidence-only rule.

**Round 3 (2026-08-13, Codex xhigh):** rounds 1-2 fixes stand; **one new finding (non-blocking, doc staleness):** §5's coverage observation ("checked-in 697-repo `stars.json`", `697 − 169 = 528`) was written against the spec-authoring snapshot while the live dataset had grown to 742 via the daily sync — the figures read as current and were not reproducible from the PR snapshot. Fixed: the observation is now explicitly marked as the 2026-08-10 authoring snapshot (742 noted at M1.2 close; M2 recomputes coverage at generation time); all four `697` occurrences swept and adjudicated — one factual claim marked historical, two illustrative examples and one sample-JSON value left as illustrations. Operational note, recorded for audit honesty: this round's dispatch produced two orphan duplicate queue tasks (a late-submitting hung round-2 dispatch and a duplicate round-3), both cancelled via the companion CLI before they could return divergent verdicts; the adjudicated round-2/round-3 results are task-msr67j82 and task-msr71b3z.

**Round 4 (2026-08-13, Codex xhigh):** three findings, all doc-precision, all reproduced and fixed. **R4-1 (Medium — a defect in the round-3 fix itself, honestly attributed):** the round-3 correction labeled the 697 figure "snapshot at spec authoring, 2026-08-10", but git history shows 697 corresponds to the **2026-07-30 dataset (`55ae7b2`)** while the spec's landing commit (`2987e92`, 2026-08-10) already carried **734** (742 at HEAD) — the date was asserted from memory, not verified; §5 now cites the commit-anchored snapshot chain (697@`55ae7b2` → 734@`2987e92` → 742 at close). **R4-2 (Low):** §3's M1.2 entry still read "(active …)" against the closed status elsewhere — now "(complete — per-slice acceptance records in §13)"; same-class sweep also fixed the stale "(this change)" labels on M0/M1.1 (pre-existing, now "(merged #234)"/"(merged #237)"). **R4-3 (Low):** the M0-SORT-2 acceptance row still showed `?sort=name_with_owner&direction=asc`, stale since M1.1's default-direction omission (M1.1's own §-note says the TEST was updated; the table row was missed) — row now matches the shipped serializer; the §6.1 backward-compat note about redundant legacy URLs is intentional and unchanged. Kickoff 2026-09-25. This section is the M3 execution contract; §9 holds the locked design concept (Concept B). Written on `feat/m3-identity`; **the owner review gate is here** — implementation (SVG mark, favicon asset, header/CSS edits) has NOT begun.

### 14.0 Freeze anchors (immutable — do not re-litigate)

- **`M2_CLOSURE_SHA = d6b1921`** — permanent M2 closure anchor (merge commit, PR #289). Stays the closure identity even after the daily sync advances `origin/main`.
- **`M2_REVIEWED_HEAD = 338f8c9`** — the reviewed head that merged as `d6b1921`.
- **`M3_BASE_SHA = d6b1921`** — fresh `origin/main` at kickoff. **Post-M2 drift = 0** (`git rev-list --count d6b1921..origin/main` = 0; the daily sync has not advanced `main` since closure). `feat/m3-identity` cut from `origin/main@d6b1921`, NOT from any milestone branch.
- **Frozen-open / OUT (M3 absorbs none):** F4, F15, R8-B, R12-5, and the three accepted staging residuals; **`skills-staging/` stays untouched**. M2.5 is CLOSED — oracle / loader / classification semantics are NOT reopened.

### 14.1 Orientation (2026-09-25, observed on `d6b1921`)

1. **Base + drift** — `origin/main == d6b1921` post-fetch; drift 0 (above). Remote is `F-e-u-e-r/starledger` ⇒ **the GitHub repo is `starledger`** (the local dir name differs), which sets the Pages base to `/starledger/`.
2. **Header DOM** — `RepositoryView.tsx:230` `<header class="dashboard-head"> → <div class="brand-row"> → <div><h1>StarLedger</h1><p>…</p></div>` + a right-aligned `.dataset-status`. The wordmark is the `<h1>` text; **no SVG mark today**. The mark inserts into the left block, before the `<h1>`.
3. **`--accent` / `--fg`** — `src/styles.css`: `:root` light `--accent:#0969da`, `--fg:#1f2328`, `--bg:#fff`; `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` `--accent:#4493f8`, `--fg:#e6edf3`, `--bg:#0d1117`; `color-scheme: light dark`. Theme is **media-query only — no manual switcher** (so "new light/dark switcher" is correctly OUT). **No `--brand` var and no `.brand-mark` rule exist yet** (both net-new).
4. **`index.html`** — `<title>StarLedger</title>` present; **no favicon**. Ships a pinned CSP meta (`img-src 'self' data:` among others), asserted per-directive by `packages/deploy/src/verify.ts`.
5. **Vite base / Pages** — `vite.config.ts`: `base = GITHUB_ACTIONS && repo ? '/${repo}/' : '/'`, `repo` from `GITHUB_REPOSITORY`. Local build ⇒ base `/` (the current `dist/index.html` shows `/assets/…`); CI ⇒ base `/starledger/` (Actions auto-sets `GITHUB_REPOSITORY=F-e-u-e-r/starledger`). `pages.yml`: build → `deploy stage`/`verify`/`smoke` → upload `apps/dashboard/dist` as the Pages artifact root.
6. **Tests / a11y** — `RepositoryView.test.tsx:58` already asserts `getByRole('heading',{name:'StarLedger'})` (the a11y anchor the mark must not disturb). Base-path-in-JS is tested via `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` stubs (`skills-projection.test.tsx`, `load-stars.test.ts`) — but the favicon lives in **static index.html**, whose base resolves at **build time**, a different mechanism. No favicon/logo/svg test exists (all net-new).
7. **Static-asset convention** — **no `apps/dashboard/public/` dir today**; no tracked svg/ico/png in the app. Vite's default public dir is the convention-consistent home: `apps/dashboard/public/favicon.svg` → copied unhashed to `dist/favicon.svg` → served at `/starledger/favicon.svg`.

**CSP coherence (pinned):** a same-origin `/favicon.svg` is allowed by `img-src 'self' data:`; an external favicon would be blocked and a `data:` URI is CSP-legal but owner-forbidden — so a **real committed same-origin asset** (M3-ID-4) is the only choice satisfying both the CSP and §9. Adding a `<link rel="icon">` does **not** touch the CSP meta ⇒ `verify.ts`'s exact-CSP assertion stays green; **implementation must not edit the CSP.**

### 14.2 The base-path pit (the one to pin)

§9 writes `/favicon.svg`; under the CI base `/starledger/` the favicon must resolve to the **project-site** root. **`verify.ts` does not catch a favicon mistake:** PATH-2 only checks that `/assets/` URLs are base-prefixed, and `staticSmoke` resolves only a fixed set (index.html + one asset + the two data files) — **neither inspects the favicon.** So M3-ID-5 is genuinely net-new, executable coverage, not a by-product of an existing gate.

- **Candidate mechanism:** Vite's `%BASE_URL%` token in index.html (`href="%BASE_URL%favicon.svg"`), replaced with the resolved base at build. This is the **pinned source mechanism** (favicon-wiring source-guard); the **built output** is the real acceptance (M3-ID-5).
- **F-F — resolved 2026-09-25 (executable, §14.10).** An earlier draft here called a hardcoded root `/favicon.svg` "wrong on Pages". That is **overstated.** A disposable build (source forced to `/favicon.svg`, `GITHUB_ACTIONS=1 GITHUB_REPOSITORY=F-e-u-e-r/starledger`) emitted `dist/index.html` with `href="/starledger/favicon.svg"` — Vite's `vite:build-html` **rebases root-absolute public URLs too** (index.html restored byte-clean after the probe). So M3 does **not** depend on root-absolute rewriting; `%BASE_URL%` is pinned only because it is explicit, and acceptance is by the built OUTPUT, not the token.

**IN:** header brand mark (inline ledger+star SVG, Concept B) · StarLedger wordmark styling · `favicon.svg` asset · index.html favicon wiring · narrowly-required CSS (`.brand-mark` + minimal `.brand-row` layout) · tests for identity / a11y / base-path correctness · P7 status freeze + this M3 record.

**OUT:** filtering / search / pagination / state · classification, M2 loader or artifacts · theme redesign · a new light/dark switcher · navigation / layout redesign · F4 / F15 / R8-B / R12-5 · staging-residual cleanup · old-branch cleanup.

### 14.5 Favicon standalone colour — RESOLVED: F-1 (owner ruling 2026-09-25)

**Resolved 2026-09-25 — F-1** (single static `#0969da`; implemented, §14.8; oracle lock DONE §14.10). Options retained below for the record.

An external favicon does **not** inherit the page's `currentColor`, so the in-page `--accent` mechanism (M3-ID-2) does not reach it. Two narrow options against the observed tokens (light `#0969da` / dark `#4493f8`):

- **F-1 — single static fill (recommended).** One fixed accent fill (e.g. light-accent `#0969da`), legible on both light and dark browser chrome. Universal rendering, zero risk, and the mark's real job is 16 px legibility.
- **F-2 — theme-responsive.** Embed `<style>@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){…}</style>` in the SVG, swapping `#0969da`⇄`#4493f8` to mirror the page tokens. Matches page theme-awareness, but SVG-favicon media-query support is browser-inconsistent; it degrades to the light fill (still legible), so it is a progressive enhancement, not a correctness risk.

No wordmark/theme redesign either way — this stays within §14.3 IN.

**Reproduced oracle gaps** (executable, backup-restore method — each mutation leaves the suite GREEN; verified then reverted byte-clean to `974ca5b7…`):

- **F-A (M3-ID-3), 3-leg** — a role-less second accessible "StarLedger" slips through: `<span class="visually-hidden">StarLedger</span>` beside the heading ⇒ RepositoryView.test 49/49 green (the oracle checks one heading + no img role, NOT accessible-name UNIQUENESS). **R2 (Grok-4.7@high on the R1-amend candidate) = FIX×5, all author-reproduced.** The R1-strengthened oracles still had _adjacent_ escapes: (E1) F-E was presence-only — an appended `color:red` or a 2nd `.brand-mark` rule wins at runtime; (E2) F-C never inspected the ROOT `<svg>` — `style="color:#f0f"` on it repaints every currentColor child; (E3) F-C allowed default-black on non-enumerated shapes (`polyline`/`text`) and a child `<style>`; (E4) F-D locked HEX only — `fill="black"`/`rgb()` escaped; (E5) the WORDMARK cascade (`.brand-identity h1{color:var(--accent)}`) was unguarded. Sol/Luna were codex-quota-blocked this round (missing lens, not a verdict — recorded, not counted). First remediated by strengthening the same-shape oracles (root+all-elements, FILLABLE set, {#0969da,none} lock, unique-rule+winning-color) — MUTATION-PROVEN 14/14 (R1 set + E1–E4) — but that was still the wrong altitude:

**R3 (Grok-4.7@high on that rebuild) = FIX again, all reproduced.** The deeper class: regex/attribute _source-text_ checks cannot parse CSS/HTML/SVG, and SVG paint has many channels. New escapes — F-E `/color:/` matches `background-color`/comments/ignores `!important`, and `.brand-mark /*x*/{…}` hides a 2nd rule from `\.brand-mark\s*\{`; F-C misses the `color` presentation attribute (`<g color="red">`), `oklch()`, `<filter feFlood>`, `<image>`; F-D reads only double-quoted paints (`fill='black'` escapes); F-B sees only `rel="…"` (single-/unquoted escape); F-A misses `aria-labelledby` (a `role=group aria-labelledby` exposes a 2nd accessible "StarLedger"). Grok itself named the convergent fix: assert the USED `fill`/`stroke`/`color` of the painted shapes, not the container's `color`.

**Owner ruling (2026-09-25): treat the five as ONE must-fix family; replace the verification MECHANISM (a narrow parser/browser two-layer gate), do NOT keep hardening regex hole-by-hole.** Production bytes unchanged — the four production blobs stay `1492abf0…e478`, byte-identical to R1; only the two test files move. ### 14.12 Pre-commit review R4 — owner adjudicates the FINAL boundary; visibility + canonical-geometry mechanisms (2026-09-25)

**R4 (Grok-4.7@high on the R3-remediation candidate `ce8a8e7f…`) = FIX×3, all author-reproduced.** The parser/browser gate — complete for colour SEMANTICS, structure, a11y and destination — is still blind to what happens to PIXELS after the computed-property sample: (1) wordmark `-webkit-text-fill-color`/`::first-line`; (2) mark `opacity:0`/`visibility:hidden`/`fill-opacity:0`/`stroke-opacity:0`/`filter`; (3) geometry — a different `path d`, a line-endpoint drift, or `stroke-width:0`. Reproduced in-browser: each left `computed color/fill/stroke` (and the AT name count) green while the rendered pixels changed (observed `svg opacity 0`, `path fill-opacity 0`, `h1 -webkit-text-fill-color rgb(255,0,0)`).

**Owner adjudication (2026-09-25): R4 correctly falsified the prior over-broad "final rendered paint" boundary — `getComputedStyle().color/fill/stroke` is not pixel composition. Rather than extend an UNBOUNDED property enumeration (filter → mask → clip-path → blend-mode → pseudo-element → …, i.e. a general-purpose visual-regression system), the owner adjudicated the BOUNDARY.** §14.11's wording ("parsed DOM / accessibility semantics / final rendered paint"; "a mutation that changes final rendered paint is a blocker") is **superseded** by:

> **FINAL M3 acceptance boundary = parsed identity semantics + accessibility semantics + canonical mark geometry + token-resolved base paint + ordinary visibility.** M3 guarantees: favicon destination / same-origin / Pages base; exactly one accessible `StarLedger` in the header; the inline mark and favicon are the canonical Concept-B geometry; the mark's base paint resolves `currentColor → --accent` and the wordmark `color → --fg`; the mark and wordmark are not suppressed in ordinary CSS rendering. It does **NOT** guarantee pixel-identical rasterisation or any arbitrary CSS compositing path.
>
> **Load-bearing blocker rule:** _A blocker must violate one of the explicitly enumerated M3 invariants — parsed wiring/identity, accessibility semantics, canonical geometry, token-resolved base paint, or ordinary visibility — while all corresponding gates remain green. Pixel differences produced solely through compositing mechanisms outside those enumerated invariants are NOT M3 blockers._
>
> **Amendment (R15, 2026-09-25 — owner-ratified; see §14.22):** the enumerated (iii) "canonical mark geometry" includes, for the three lines, `stroke-linecap: round` alongside the endpoints and `stroke-width: 1.6` — it is authored identically in both shipped Concept-B representations (inline mark + favicon) and materially defines their canonical rounded-line appearance. This does NOT widen M3 to arbitrary stroke styling: `stroke-dasharray`, `stroke-linejoin`, filters, masks, transforms and other compositing/styling channels remain OUT.
>
> **Amendment (R16, 2026-09-26 — owner-ratified STATE UNIVERSE; see §14.23):** For browser-used invariants, M3's state universe is the at-rest screen rendering under the application's two shipped persistent `prefers-color-scheme` states: light and dark. Transient pseudo-class states (`:hover`, `:focus`, `:active`) and alternate rendering environments (`print`, `forced-colors`) are outside M3 unless a future product contract explicitly defines identity behavior for them. A reviewer raising a `:hover`/`:focus`/`:active`/`print`/`forced-colors`-only override is `REJECTED-WITH-REASON / outside ratified state universe`, not a new round.

**R4 finding disposition (owner-set):**

| R4 finding                                                                                | disposition                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `opacity:0`, `visibility:hidden`, shape `fill-opacity:0`/`stroke-opacity:0`               | **MUST-FIX oracle** — "renders a brand mark" cannot allow invisibility |
| `path d`, line endpoints, `stroke-width:0` (canonical-shape mutation)                     | **MUST-FIX oracle** — identity geometry IS the Concept-B contract      |
| `filter`, `-webkit-text-fill-color`, `::first-line`, arbitrary blend/clip/mask/pseudo/etc | **VALID observation / OUT OF M3 ACCEPTANCE** — no pixel-golden system  |

**No screenshot / pixel-golden oracle for M3 (explicit owner ruling).** It would turn this identity slice into raster-level visual regression (fonts, AA, browser/platform rendering, device scale all enter the gate) — cost and false-positive surface disproportionate to a ~30 px decorative mark. A visual-regression system, if ever wanted, is a separate architecture decision, not smuggled into M3.

**Two mechanisms added (the MUST-FIX pair), each falsification-proven:**

- **Visibility envelope (browser gate, light+dark):** `mark` and `wordmark` — `display != none`, `visibility == visible`, `opacity == 1`; painted shapes — `fill-opacity == 1`, `stroke-opacity == 1`. Deliberately NOT extended to filter/mask/clip/blend/text-fill. Proven: correct ENVELOPE_PASS; `.brand-mark{opacity:0}` → markVisible **false**; `{visibility:hidden}` → **false**; `.brand-mark path{fill-opacity:0}` → fillOpOK **false**; `.brand-identity h1{opacity:0}` → wordVisible **false**.
- **Canonical geometry (vitest, parsed descriptor — quote style / attribute ordering irrelevant, NOT a raw-string golden):** the inline mark and favicon pin `svg viewBox`; `rect` x/y/width/height/rx + stroke-width; `path` exact `d`; `line ×3` exact x1/y1/x2/y2 + stroke-width. Proven RED: `path d` drift (inline + favicon); line `stroke-width=0`; line-endpoint drift.

**Falsification matrix — this round adds V/G to the retained A/B/C/C2/D/E/E2 (all predicted-then-observed):**

| —   | `filter` / `-webkit-text-fill-color` / `::first-line` / blend/clip/mask | —                | **REJECTED-WITH-BOUNDARY** (valid, outside ratified M3) |

### 14.13 Pre-commit review R5 — completing the enumerated-invariant gates (checkVisibility + used geometry + media) (2026-09-25)

**R5 (Grok-4.7@high on the R4 candidate `c4c8cfa9…`) = FIX×4, all author-reproduced, all IN-BOUNDARY.** Grok correctly set aside filter/`-webkit-text-fill-color`/`::first-line`/blend/clip/mask/shadow/transform (the §14.12 out-of-M3 set) and instead found that the gates _claiming_ the enumerated invariants under-sampled them:

- **(v) visibility — wrong boxes.** The R4 envelope read `opacity`/`visibility`/`display` on the `<svg>` and `h1` only, and on shapes only `fill-opacity`/`stroke-opacity`. So a **shape-level** `opacity:0`/`visibility:hidden`/`display:none`, or an **ancestor** `opacity:0` (opacity groups, it does not inherit, so each element's own computed opacity stays 1), suppressed the mark/wordmark while the envelope stayed green.
- **(iii) geometry — source vs used.** vitest pinned the presentation **attributes** (`getAttribute`), so a CSS `stroke-width:0` (or a CSS `d`/coord override) changed the **used** geometry while the attribute — hence vitest — stayed green.
- **(i) wiring — media.** F-B checked the parsed `href` but not `media`: a `media="not all"` icon link parses fine (one `rel~="icon"`, correct href) yet the browser never applies it, dropping the favicon destination.

**Owner rule (in-boundary breach ⇒ blocker) ⇒ MUST-FIX. This does NOT move the §14.12 boundary — it completes the GATES that enforce it.** The fixes are convergent (they read the invariant's own final value, not a proxy), not new invariants:

- **Visibility → `Element.checkVisibility({opacityProperty, visibilityProperty, contentVisibilityAuto})`** on the mark, the wordmark, AND every painted shape (the browser's own "is it actually visible" test — it accounts for ancestors and shape-level `opacity`/`visibility`/`display`/content-visibility), plus `fill-opacity`/`stroke-opacity == 1` on the shapes.

**With this, the FULL parsed tree — namespace + vocabulary + exact multiset + exact per-element attribute maps + direct-child parentage + leaf shapes — is pinned for BOTH the inline mark and the favicon.** No parsed-tree dimension (structure, position, attributes) remains for a mutation to change while a gate stays green. **R14 = 3-leg on the R14 candidate.** Grok-4.7@high re-dispatched now; `gpt-5.6-sol@max` → `gpt-5.6-luna@max` at the 20:12 codex reset. Browser-verified on the mark: `getComputedStyle(line).x1` is absent (`(none)`), and injecting `.brand-mark line{x1:4px;y1:12px;x2:20px;y2:12px}` leaves `x1/x2/y1.baseVal` and `getBBox()` unchanged (`endpointsChanged=false`) — the rule has zero effect on the used geometry. F-C's source-attribute pin on the line endpoints is therefore complete (no CSS override channel exists). **Zero surviving in-boundary finding ⇒ the R14 candidate `eb8e50ec…37df` is Grok-CLEAN and FROZEN** (first Grok round with no valid finding since R8's PROCEED — the whole parsed tree + used geometry/paint/visibility are pinned). `gpt-5.6-sol@max` → `gpt-5.6-luna@max` run at the 20:12 codex reset on this SAME candidate for the final 3/3; closure holds only on 3/3 literal PROCEED + zero surviving in-boundary.

The shipped inline mark and favicon both author `stroke-linecap="round"`, so the two Concept-B representations were asymmetric: an inline `round → butt`, a dropped attribute, or a CSS cascade `.brand-mark line { stroke-linecap: butt }` in the app's own stylesheet would leave F-C green while the canonical rounded-line appearance changed.

**Owner adjudication (2026-09-25): linecap is IN — a valid, convergent in-boundary finding → FIX at R15 (candidate changes, so R14's Grok-CLEAN + these two findings do NOT compose into closure).** The favicon already treats `stroke-linecap: round` as part of its canonical descriptor and both shipped representations author it identically, so it is a canonical (iii) **line** invariant, not arbitrary stroke styling — the earlier "same class as `stroke-dasharray` ⇒ OUT" reasoning is overruled (dasharray stays OUT; linecap is IN because it is authored identically in both representations and materially defines the rounded-line appearance). The fix must close BOTH the source and the CSS-cascade channel — a lone source assertion would reopen the source-vs-used gap that stroke-width/geometry already taught (§14.13–14.14). Full remediation record §14.22.
