### 14.22 Pre-commit review R14 closure → R15 remediation — `stroke-linecap` pinned IN at both layers (source + used); boundary tightened (2026-09-25)

**Owner ruling applied (R14 Sol/Luna convergent finding → R15 fix).** `stroke-linecap: round` is now an enumerated (iii) canonical Concept-B **line** invariant for the inline mark, matching the favicon (which already pins it). Two channels, per the owner's explicit two-arm requirement:

- **Layer 1 — SOURCE pin (F-C, `RepositoryView.test.tsx`).** The inline-mark line descriptor grows from a 5-tuple to a **6-tuple** `[x1, y1, x2, y2, stroke-width, stroke-linecap]`, each expected `…, '1.6', 'round'`. Catches `round → butt`, a dropped attribute, or an endpoint drift at the parsed-source level (quote-style / ordering irrelevant).
- **Layer 2 — USED pin (browser gate, extends §14.14's used-geometry procedure).** The browser gate now also reads `getComputedStyle(line).strokeLinecap` on all three `.brand-mark line`s and asserts `== ['round','round','round']`. This closes the CSS-cascade channel jsdom cannot resolve (`.brand-mark line { stroke-linecap: butt }` in the app's own same-origin stylesheet — the ONLY channel CSP `style-src 'self'` leaves open; injected inline `<style>` / `style=` are CSP-blocked).

**The favicon needs no change** — F-D's exact parsed attribute map already includes `stroke-linecap: round` on each line (plus root attrs, parentage, leaf). R15 only brings the inline representation to the same contract level.

**Boundary (§14.12) tightened — owner-ratified:** _Canonical line geometry/style consists of the three fixed endpoints, `stroke-width: 1.6`, and `stroke-linecap: round`. `stroke-linecap` is IN because it is authored identically in both shipped Concept-B representations and materially defines their canonical rounded-line appearance. This does NOT expand M3 to arbitrary stroke styling; `stroke-dasharray`, joins (`stroke-linejoin`), filters, masks, transforms and other previously out-of-boundary compositing/styling channels remain OUT (§14.12)._

**Mutation proof — two channels, three arms (predicted-then-observed; production files `cp`-restored byte-clean each time, never `git checkout`; §14.9 lesson):**

**The fix (mechanism, not a patch):** the browser gate's OUTERMOST layer is now an explicit two-state loop — `for (const colorScheme of ['light','dark']) { await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme }); await page.reload(); …assert the full used-contract… }` — so paint (iv), visibility (v), used geometry/stroke-width (iii) AND the new linecap (iii) are all driven by the SAME state matrix. The point is not "sample dark linecap once" but that no future used invariant can again be accidentally sampled in only one scheme. Playwright is deliberately NOT committed as a test this round (the whole M3 arc keeps the browser gate a verification procedure; introducing test infrastructure now would expand scope) — the durable two-scheme procedure + evidence artifact is the gate.

- **Mutation proof (two arms — the second state is necessary AND discriminating):** with `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { .brand-mark line { stroke-linecap: butt } }` in styles.css (rebuilt + restaged; `cp`-restored byte-clean after) ⇒ **light lineCaps `[round,round,round]` PASS, dark lineCaps `[butt,butt,butt]` RED** (killed by the dark used-style gate). **Diagnosis (operational-rigor §5, three-defects-one-mechanism): findings 1 & 2 are covered by the ESTABLISHED §14.13 gate** — "`checkVisibility` on the mark, the wordmark, AND every painted shape" + "fill-opacity/stroke-opacity == 1 on the shapes" already includes rect + path. **The recurring mechanism is that the browser-EVIDENCE ARTIFACT keeps being a hand-picked subset of what the gate actually asserts** (R15 dark linecap → R16 rect-sw/path-d/wordmark → R17 rect/path-visibility + rect-stroke-opacity), so each round a reviewer points at the un-serialized field. Finding 3 (per-scheme AT) is a genuinely new small dimension. Patching each field again invites R18/R19; the fix is to REPLACE the mechanism (one COMPLETE systematic capture: every element × every visibility/opacity/paint/geometry channel × both schemes + per-scheme AT name-count) — OR the owner applies the §14.12/R4 disproportionality principle ("no adversarial hardening disproportionate to a ~30 px decorative mark") and declares the established gate suite sufficient. **This is exactly the owner-reserved "gate sufficient" ruling ⇒ ESCALATED; no auto-R18.** Candidate frozen (CODE `cce730ca…`, production `1492abf0…`); commit / PR / merge stay owner actions.

### 14.25 Pre-commit review R17 escalation → R18 — the evidence MECHANISM replaced by a contract-driven browser gate (2026-09-26)

**Owner ruling (2026-09-26): Option A′ — do NOT patch the three R17 fields; REPLACE the mechanism with a single contract-driven collector that is mechanically complete _relative to its CONTRACT_, and fingerprint the gate procedure itself.** Applied in full, plus one author correction to the root-cause framing (below). _Wording note (owner, tightened before dispatch):_ the completeness guard is complete **relative to the CONTRACT** — it proves every CONTRACT field is collected/asserted and every owned invariant (ii/iii/iv/v) carries ≥1 field; it **cannot** self-prove that a subfield which _ought_ to belong to an invariant is not missing from the CONTRACT itself. That residual is exactly why the final reviewer still performs a semantic comparison — and it is exactly the gap the owner's base-paint amendment (below) closed before dispatch.

**Amendment (2026-09-26, before dispatch — owner base-paint MUST-FIX; handled in R18, no R19).** Reviewing the frozen 40-field candidate the owner found one in-boundary omission: the CONTRACT pinned each shape's **active** base-paint channel (rect→stroke, path→fill) but never the **inactive** one, so a dark `rect{fill:…}` (fills the hollow ledger frame) or `path{stroke:…}` (strokes the star) — a token-resolved **invariant (iv)** base-paint change, NOT a filter/mask/pixel effect — passed the 40-field gate all-green. **Fix:** add `rect.fill===none` + `path.stroke===none` as invariant (iv); inactive-channel OPACITY stays N/A (channel pinned `none` ⇒ no painted fill/stroke for an opacity to act on); no `path.strokeWidth` (stroke===none ⇒ stroke-width unobservable). **40 → 42 fields; iv coverage 7 → 9.** Re-proven by new arms **B1/B2** (each with a differential old-gate-blind control), whole matrix re-run under the amended gate, GATE/EVIDENCE fingerprints re-frozen. Values below are the **amended (42-field)** state.

**Root-cause correction (honest, worse than "a JSON missing 3 fields").** The escalation diagnosis (§14.24) said findings 1 & 2 were "covered by the ESTABLISHED §14.13 gate" and only the evidence artifact under-serialized them. Verified against the artifacts, this does not hold: **there was no durable gate distinct from the evidence** — the browser gate was an ad-hoc Playwright PROCEDURE re-typed each round, and the JSON was the ONLY durable record of what executed. So for any round, the set of fields ASSERTED equalled the set SERIALIZED; R17 did not assert-then-drop rect/path visibility + rect stroke-opacity — **it never asserted them.** Production is unaffected (the mutations are hypothetical; real CSS has none), so delta stays 0, but the "entire used-contract verified both schemes" claim was over-stated three rounds running. The recurring mechanism = gate/assertion/evidence were three hand-maintained lists that drifted; the fix is to make them **one** list.

**The fix — `m3-contract-gate.mjs` (durable, fingerprinted).** One `CONTRACT` map (42 flat `subject.field` entries — 40 in the first freeze + the 2 base-paint fields of the amendment — each with its per-scheme expected value + the invariant it pins) drives, in order: `collect(observed) → assert(observed===expect) → completenessGuard → serialize`. The asserted set, collected set, and serialized set are ONE set by construction — no hand-picked subset can drift. Five reinforcements:

- **Single source of truth** — `collect`, `assert`, and the evidence all iterate the one `CONTRACT`.
- **Bidirectional completeness guard** — (A) schema↔collection (every contract field collected; nothing off-contract) catches the R15→R17 hand-picked-subset drift; (B) invariant↔schema (every owned invariant ii/iii/iv/v covered by ≥1 field; every field maps to an owned invariant) catches the owner-named _true blocker_ "an invariant with no schema field" that a key-set check alone cannot see. Both directions **mutation-proven falsifiable**. (Limit, per the wording note above: neither direction can detect a subfield that _ought_ to be in the CONTRACT but is absent — the base-paint gap — which is the reviewer's semantic job, not the guard's.)
- **Gate procedure fingerprint** — the executed script is a durable file, hashed. **R18 identity = CODE fp + GATE fp + EVIDENCE fp**, binding the evidence to the gate that produced it (a hand-produced JSON can no longer stand in for a gate that never ran).
- **Per-scheme accessibility via the real AT** — `headerAT.starLedgerCount` read from `locator('.dashboard-head').ariaSnapshot()` (the accessibility tree), both schemes — not from `::after` computed content.
- **Base-paint completeness (owner amendment)** — invariant (iv) pins BOTH channels of each shape: `rect.stroke===accent` **and `rect.fill===none`**; `path.fill===accent` **and `path.stroke===none`** — so painting the normally-inactive channel is caught (arms B1/B2).

Owned invariants (browser-USED half): (ii)(iii)(iv)(v). Explicitly owned ELSEWHERE, named not dropped: (i) favicon link/media/type → vitest F-B + build; line endpoints x1/y1/x2/y2 + viewBox (not CSS-settable, SVG2) → F-C source; favicon asset exactness → F-D. NA channels (rect.fillOpacity, path.strokeOpacity, line×3.fillOpacity — the SVG paints rect+lines via stroke only, path via fill only) are observed + serialized (`1`) but not asserted, each with a recorded reason.

**Evidence (built bundle via `vite preview` 4188, `page.emulateMedia` + reload + getComputedStyle + checkVisibility + ariaSnapshot):**

- **Baseline both schemes = PASS** — `pass:true`, `darkSchemeConfirmed:true`; each scheme 0 failures, guard PASS, **42==42 fields**, coverage `{ii:2,iii:14,iv:9,v:17}`, lineCount 3. `rect.fill` and `path.stroke` observed `none` in both schemes.
- **Six independent faithful mutation arms** (styles.css append → build → stage → reload → re-run the SAME 42-field gate; `cp`-restored byte-clean 87e623bd + delta-0 1492abf0 re-verified each time; never `git checkout`):

### 14.26 Pre-commit review R18 result → R19 — invariant-(ii) oracle remediation (exact accessible-name oracles) (2026-09-27)

**R18 3-leg result (fresh, on the re-frozen 42-field candidate; recorded as-is, NOT CLOSED):** Grok-4.7@high **PROCEED** (verified all four fps + gate==Appendix A; "no invariant wrongly evaluated" — missed the finding); `gpt-5.6-sol@max` **FIX**; `gpt-5.6-luna@max` **FIX** (convergent — but Luna ran in the shared `r18-review/` workdir where Sol's verdict already existed and read it, so its convergence is not fully independent; Sol's independent finding + the author reproduction stand regardless). **Finding:** invariant (ii) "exactly one accessible StarLedger in the header" was evaluated by two oracles both blind to CSS generated content on the wordmark `<h1>` — `wordmark.text` = `h1.textContent` (excludes `::before`/`::after`) and `headerAT.starLedgerCount` = `/StarLedger/g` **substring** count over the ariaSnapshot — so a dark `.brand-identity h1::after{content:"X"}` set the wordmark's **computed accessible name** to `StarLedgerX` while the gate stayed `pass:true`. **Author-reproduced on the built bundle** (`r18-review/R18-repro-ii-accessible-name.md`: baseline `heading "StarLedger"`, arm `heading "StarLedgerX"`, gate green both times; cp-restored, delta-0 + CODE fp re-verified). ⇒ NOT 3/3 PROCEED; escalated with recommendation (a) must-fix.

**Owner ruling (2026-09-27): VALID / MUST-FIX; option (b) reject refused** — with an executable counterexample in hand, rejecting would silently downgrade (ii) from "exactly one EXACT accessible StarLedger" to "accessible text contains StarLedger somewhere", i.e. a contract change. **Root cause = an existing invariant INCORRECTLY EVALUATED (oracle too loose), not a missing CONTRACT field and not a new invariant.** Open **R19** as a **narrow (ii) oracle remediation only**, with new round provenance (R18 was actually dispatched with a PROCEED/FIX/FIX history, and changing the executable gate creates a new candidate identity); R18 stays recorded as "NOT CLOSED → finding reproduced → superseded by R19". (The §14.25 phrase "handled in R18, no R19" refers to the base-paint amendment only.) Also ruled: the §14.25 prettier table padding is accepted (mechanical alignment, no identity touched); no additional paint/geometry/state expansion; no voluntary R20 hardening; no commit yet.

**The fix — two complementary EXACT oracles under (ii), `m3-contract-gate.mjs` (R19).** `headerAT.starLedgerCount` (substring) is replaced by: **`headerAT.headingName`** — the wordmark heading's computed accessible name, read from the `<h1>`'s OWN `ariaSnapshot()` node, must be **exactly** `"StarLedger"` (catches `::after` suffix / `::before` prefix corruption, and shows the corrupted name diagnostically); **`headerAT.starLedgerExactCount`** — the number of accessibility-tree nodes in `.dashboard-head` whose accessible **name** or **text** is **exactly** `"StarLedger"`, must be **1** (retains the A1 duplicate case; `"StarLedgerX"`/`"XStarLedger"` count 0, so suffix/prefix corruption and the A1 duplicate are separately identifiable). Deliberately NOT collapsed into a single heading-name check — that would re-lose `.dashboard-head::after{content:"StarLedger"}` (heading name intact, second identity present). `wordmark.text === "StarLedger"` **stays** (DOM/source-visible text, a different layer; it cannot replace the accessible-name oracle — it stayed `"StarLedger"` on the C1 bundle). **Exactness mechanism:** the ariaSnapshot YAML is parsed with the installed renderer's grammar (playwright-core 1.64.0-alpha `ariaSnapshotRenderer.ts` + `yaml.ts`: `- text: VALUE`; `- KEY`/`- KEY:`/`- KEY: VALUE`; `- /prop: VALUE`; `KEY = role [JSON.stringify(name)] [ [attr]… ]`, whole key `'…'`-quoted with `''` escapes when YAML needs it); names/values are unescaped then compared with `===` only — no regex on content anywhere. **Fail-closed:** any line outside the grammar lands in `axUnparsedLines` and a new completeness-guard direction **(C) `axParseOk`** fails the guard, so an unreadable identity-bearing line can never silently lower the exact count; (C) is oracle integrity for the existing (ii) fields, **not a new invariant** (`OWNED_INVARIANTS` unchanged). Over-counting is the fail-closed direction. The parser block is marker-delimited and unit-falsified standalone (`ax-parser-unit.mjs` loads it VERBATIM from the gate: 13 semantic cases + 5 malformed-line cases, each with the OLD substring oracle's answer alongside — ALL HOLD). **42 → 43 fields; (ii) coverage 2 → 3; nothing else in the CONTRACT changed** (other 41 fields, ACCENT/FG/PATH_D, collector, guard A/B, light/dark loop byte-identical to R18). **No production or test source changed.**

### 14.27 Pre-commit review R19 result → R20 — invariant-(ii) SOURCE remediation (Chrome CDP accessibility tree replaces `ariaSnapshot()`) (2026-09-27)

**Owner ruling on the R19 leg-1 finding (2026-09-27): VALID / MUST-FIX. R19 NOT CLOSED. GO R20 — narrow invariant-(ii) SOURCE remediation only.** Six points, all binding: (1) Chrome CDP Accessibility data is the **sole authoritative (ii) source** — `headerAT.headingName` ← Chrome's computed name of the `<h1>`'s AX node, `headerAT.starLedgerExactCount` ← the header's Chrome AX subtree; the R19 parser is no longer an oracle; Option 2 (pinning `content:none/normal`) **rejected**. (2) Do NOT write `queryAXTree(...).length === 1` or any count rule before **empirically mapping baseline + A1 raw AX topology** — raw AX-node cardinality is not identity cardinality; semantics = `headingName === "StarLedger"` AND, apart from the wordmark `<h1>` and its AX descendants, no other exact `StarLedger` accessible identity in the header. (3) Delete guard (C) and everything parser-specific (`parseAx`, `axUnparsedLines`, `ax-parser-unit.mjs`, `axParseOk`); no C′ field unless CDP errors are caught to still emit JSON — a throwing collector with a non-zero gate already IS fail-closed; a measurement failure must never become a canonical value; no `ariaSnapshot()` retained even as a diagnostic. **Step 1 — empirical Chrome AX topology BEFORE any count rule (`r20-review/R20-ax-topology-and-count-rule.md`, fp `b3cf2dbd…`; dumps `topology/{baseline,A1,C1,G1}.topology.json` via `ax-topology-dump.mjs` + `topology-arms.sh`, Chrome 153 headless `channel:'chrome'`, each arm cp-restored `87e623bd` + delta-0):** (a) **one rendered identity = up to THREE raw non-ignored AX nodes** carrying the same name — baseline header subtree = `sectionheader "" › none(ignored) › none(ignored) › none(ignored) › heading "StarLedger" › StaticText "StarLedger" › InlineTextBox "StarLedger"` (+ the two paragraphs) = 17 nodes; (b) `Accessibility.queryAXTree(header, accessibleName:"StarLedger")` returns the semantic node AND its StaticText: baseline **2**, A1 3, C1 1 ⇒ rejected as the oracle; (c) `getPartialAXTree(node, fetchRelatives:false)` returns exactly the element's own node (`name.type computedString`, `backendDOMNodeId` = the DOM node) and its ids are consistent with `getFullAXTree` under `Accessibility.enable`; (d) `getChildAXNodes` recursion yields the same id set as the `getFullAXTree` childIds walk but with duplicates (Chrome flattens through ignored nodes) ⇒ topology source = `getFullAXTree` + childIds walk from the header's AX id; (e) generated content on the `<h1>` (C1/G1) appears as an ignored `none` wrapper INSIDE the heading subtree with its own StaticText children while the heading's computed name changes, and the original `StaticText "StarLedger"` **survives inside the corrupted heading**; G1 renders `"X0"` yet Chrome's heading name is `"StarLedgerX"` (the counter digit is not in the name); (f) ignored nodes carry `childIds` — the walk must pass through them; (g) A1's generated `StaticText "StarLedger"` (+ InlineTextBox) is a direct child of the header's `none` wrapper, outside the `<h1>`; (h) found by the gate's own integrity guard, not the dump (`new Map` last-wins): Chrome's flat `getFullAXTree` list repeats two `InlineTextBox "·"` records (repo-card separators, outside the header) **verbatim** — 240 entries / 238 unique ids, deterministic across 3 passes and both schemes ⇒ the gate collapses a verbatim repeat and throws only on a CONFLICTING record for one id. **Candidate rules evaluated on the captures** (baseline / A1 / C1 / G1): N1/N2 count (non-)ignored exact-named nodes in S = 3/5/2/2 ✗; Q `queryAXTree().length` = 2/3/1/1 ✗; R = the owner's literal sketch (h1 exact + non-ignored exact nodes outside h1 ∪ desc) = 1/**3**/0/0 ✗ (A1 double-counts StaticText + InlineTextBox); **R′ = h1 exact + TOPMOST exact-named nodes outside h1 ∪ desc = 1/2/0/0 ✓ — frozen.**

**The fix — `m3-contract-gate.mjs` (R20), same three (ii) fields, new SOURCE.** One CDP session per run (`page.context().newCDPSession(page)` — throws in a non-Chromium browser, the intended fail-closed outcome; `Accessibility.enable` once; detached on every exit path). Per scheme after `emulateMedia` + reload: `DOM.getDocument(depth:0)` → `ownAxNode` for `.dashboard-head` and `.brand-identity h1` (`DOM.querySelector` — nodeId 0 = source failure; `DOM.describeNode`; `Accessibility.getPartialAXTree({nodeId, fetchRelatives:false})` — exactly 1 node whose `backendDOMNodeId` equals the DOM node's `backendNodeId`, else source failure) → `Accessibility.getFullAXTree({})` → `walkSubtree` from the header's AX id (every node once; unknown / twice-reached / parent-inconsistent childIds and conflicting duplicate records = source failure) → the h1's own node must lie in S and agree with its full-tree record on name/ignored/role. **`headerAT.headingName`** = the `<h1>`'s own node's `name.value` iff non-ignored and a string, else `null` (⇒ RED). **`headerAT.starLedgerExactCount`** = rule **R′**: with S = header AX subtree (root included), H = the `<h1>`'s own node, D(H) = its descendants, `exact(n) ⇔ !n.ignored && n.name === "StarLedger"`: `count = [exact(H)] + |{ n ∈ S \ ({H} ∪ D(H)) : exact(n) ∧ no ancestor a of n in S with exact(a) }|` — the wordmark contributes only through its OWN computed name (the surviving StaticText inside a corrupted heading is not a second identity); every exact identity OUTSIDE the wordmark counts once, at the top of its chain; the second term is 0 iff no exact identity exists outside the wordmark, so de-duplication can never hide one. Baseline 1; A1 2; C1/C2/G1/G2 0. The gate serializes `axTopology` (pre-order S with depth/role/name/ignored/parentId/inH1, `h1Own`, `h1Contribution`, `outsideIdentityIds`) so R′ is hand-recomputable. **Deleted, not carried:** `parseAx`, `headingAccessibleName`, the AX PARSER block, `axUnparsedLines`, `axParseOk`, guard direction (C), `headingAX`/`headerAX`, `ax-parser-unit.mjs`, `GUARD-NEGATIVE-C`. ## 15. M4.1 — Group-by-primary-category view: charter, orientation & acceptance boundary (CONTRACT FROZEN 2026-09-28 · IMPLEMENTATION FROZEN 2026-09-28 at the §15.15 checkpoint · PRE-COMMIT REVIEW CLOSED 2026-09-28 at §15.16 · M4.1 CLOSED 2026-09-28 — merged #299 as `8709be6`, terminal closure §15.17)

Kickoff 2026-09-28 (owner direction "Start M4 programme", §3: M4.1 → M4.2 → M4.3 as three separate milestones with separate review identities). This section is the M4.1 execution contract. It discharges three pre-existing reservations: §1 ("group-by-primary-category view mode **inside the same results surface**, still not a third browser"), §6 ("later `group`") and §8 ("Group-by-primary view mode uses **only** `primary_category`, so each repo appears exactly once"). Written on `feat/m4.1-group-by-category`. **Contract frozen by owner ruling 2026-09-28** — the submitted draft was approved subject to exactly two narrow amendments, both applied verbatim in semantics: **A1** M41-SRCH-1 (search enrichment unchanged; a secondary-label match still renders under its primary group; a query may yield several primary groups) and **A2** §15.11 (designated-oracle mutation rule; collateral REDs permitted and recorded). Implementation (§15.9 IN only) began after the freeze; the next owner gate is the implementation-freeze checkpoint (§15.13).

### 15.0 Freeze anchors (immutable — do not re-litigate)

- `M3_CLOSURE_SHA = 0bb7db7` (PR #297, §14.28) · `M3_DOC_CLOSURE_SHA = d1619d0` (PR #298).
- **`M4_1_BASE_SHA = d1619d0e347e200bdd67faba2bf844586ee71d9f`** — fresh `origin/main` at kickoff (`main == origin/main == HEAD`; tracked diff vs `origin/main` empty; drift since the M3 doc closure = 0). `feat/m4.1-group-by-category` cut from `origin/main@d1619d0` (`--no-track`), NOT from any milestone branch.
- **Baseline gate:** dashboard `vitest run` on `d1619d0` = **298/298** (23 files), terminal marker observed.
- **Frozen-open / OUT (M4.1 absorbs none):** F4, F15, R8-B, R12-5 and the three accepted staging residuals (§4.10 Ruling 1 last-write-wins pair concurrency · R15 crash-between-renames torn pair · §4.12 S2 pair-local stuck lock) are M4.2's subject, adjudicated there by executable evidence — never pre-decided here. `skills-staging/` (14 untracked files, fp `69830a75…`) stays byte-for-byte untouched. M2 (`d6b1921`) and M3 (`0bb7db7`) are CLOSED: classification semantics, the M2 loader/artifacts/generator and the M3 identity (SVG / favicon / accessible name) are NOT reopened.
- **Programme guardrails (owner):** preserve M0 fail-soft (§2.2); preserve M1 canonical URL/state (§6) except exactly as amended in §6 (emit order, §6.3 reset set, new §6.5) and §15.3; preserve the M2 contracts (§4, §4.11, §4.12); no unrelated deferred debt in this PR; owner-approved milestone boundaries beat "while we're here"; tests/gates count only with terminal completion markers; reviewer findings are reproduced before action; reviewer convergence is evidence, not voting; commit / push / PR / merge remain owner-gated.

### 15.1 Orientation (2026-09-28, observed on `d1619d0`)

1. **Canonical state** — `apps/dashboard/src/state/dashboard-state.ts`: one `DashboardState` (19 fields: `view`, `scope`, `query`, `sort`, `direction`, `languages`, `topics`, `licenses`, `categories`, `aiTags`, `skillCategories`, `archived`, `fork`, `stale`, `stableRelease`, `anyRelease`, `hydrationStatuses`, `density`, `page`); `normalizeDashboardState` (idempotent; invalid scalar enum → default), `parseDashboardState` (never throws; repeated scalar → last VALID), `serializeDashboardState` (defaults omitted; emit order `view, scope, skill, q, sort, direction, facets, booleans, release/hydration, density, page`). Enum lists are `as const satisfies` arrays (`VIEW_VALUES`, `SCOPE_VALUES`, `DENSITY_VALUES`); `PARAM` maps field → query key (`skillCategories → 'skill'`).
2. **State hook** — `state/use-dashboard-state.ts`: single instance owned by `App`; `PAGE_RESET_FIELDS` = view, scope, query, sort, direction, the array facets incl. `skillCategories`, the boolean/enum filters, `hydrationStatuses` (density exempt); `update(partial, mode)` resets `page → 1` only on a genuine value change and never when `page` is explicit; `reset()` = `{...DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_STATE, view: current.view, density: current.density}`; `popstate` re-parses the URL.
3. **Pipeline** — `features/repositories/select.ts`: `prepareRepositories(repos, now, annotations?, skills?, skillCategoryLabels?)` (derive + `searchText`; the skills join map is passed **only** under a coherent-ready layer) → `selectFromPrepared(prepared, view)` = search → `applyFilters` → `sortRepos` (stable, `node_id` tiebreak) → `dashboardToView(state, aiReady, skillsReady = false)` neutralizes `scope`/`skillCategories` unless ready (M24-FS-1, M24-STS-3, fail-closed). **Nothing exists after `sortRepos` today** — grouping is net-new and slots after it, outside the selector.
4. **View** — `features/repositories/RepositoryView.tsx`: `skillsReady = skillsStatus === 'ready' && skillsClassification != null` (`:123`, coherent-ready, M24-STS-1/STS-4); `results` memo (`:168`); pagination `PAGE_SIZE = 48`, `lastPage = max(1, ceil(results.length / 48))`, `effectivePage` clamp, `replace` reconciliation effect (`:177–182`, §6.2), `pageItems` slice; toolbar (`:301–368`) = search input, `Filters` button (`Filters N` = `effectiveFilterCount`), `Sort` select, direction button, `Density` select (`<label className="density"><span>Density</span><select …>`); `toolbarRef` publishes `--toolbar-h`; results `<section className="results" aria-labelledby="results-heading">` → `<h2 id="results-heading" tabIndex={-1}>Starred repositories</h2>` (focus target for chip removal / clear-all) → `FilterChips` → `<p className="result-count" role="status">` (`resultSummary`, `:55`) → AI and skills `degraded-notice` paragraphs with loading-vs-unavailable wording (`:403–417`) → `NoResults` (`results.length === 0`) or `<ul className="card-list">` + `<nav className="pager" aria-label="Pagination">` when `lastPage > 1`. `suppressedSkillsFilterCount` keeps requested-but-inert `scope`/`skill` values out of the `Filters N` count (M24-CNT-1).
5. **Card** — `features/repositories/RepositoryCard.tsx`: the title is `<h3 className="card-title">`; `DerivedRepo.skills: RepoSkillsClassification | null` (`primaryCategoryId`, `secondaryCategoryIds`, `summary`) is non-null only under a ready join; badges = primary + ≤1 secondary (M24-BDG-1).
6. **Facet UI** — `features/filters/FilterControls.tsx`: the Skills-ecosystem section (scope checkbox → `update({ scope })`, provenance note, coverage line, `.md` download, `Skill category` `CheckboxFacet` over `skills.categories` in canonical order) renders in the sidebar and in the ≤900px `FilterDrawer` (M24-DRW-1). `FilterChips.activeFilterCount(state)` = chip count.
7. **Taxonomy at runtime** — `LoadedSkillsClassification.categories` is already in canonical `order` (I-5) and every `primaryCategoryId` references a member (I-4); `coverage` is generation-time, never live-recomputed (F2 pin: `matched = 0` keeps the layer READY).
8. **Styles** — `styles.css`: `.card-list` grid `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(22rem, 1fr))`; `.density-compact .card-list` tighter gap + `minmax(min(19rem, 100%), 1fr)` at ≥901px; ≤900px single column, sidebar hidden, drawer; ≤520px toolbar static; `.view-tabs` sticky; no `content-visibility`.
9. **Live data (root artifacts, 2026-09-28)** — `stars.json` 866 repos; classification 24 categories / 171 entries (166 resolved, 5 unresolved; generated 2026-08-13 against 744 repos, `unclassified_repo_count` 578 at generation time); **live join 165** (one resolved `node_id` is no longer starred) ⇒ 701 live repos unclassified; 38 entries carry a secondary; largest primary bucket 31 (`mcp-integrations`); domain categories `order` 0–17, infrastructure 18–23.
10. **Why the existing model does not suffice** — `view` is the tab, `density` a row preference, `scope` a narrowing; none expresses "grouped presentation". Bookmark / back-forward restoration is required and parallel local UI-only state is forbidden ⇒ one new canonical field (§15.3).

### 15.2 Product intent & non-goals (owner)

Grouped-by-primary-category browsing **inside the existing Starred results surface**. Starred remains the only canonical repo browser; Skills remains an optional classification layer. **NOT:** a third browser · a Markdown taxonomy reader · duplicated cards across primary + secondary · a classification or data-contract redesign · an M2 debt-cleanup vehicle · a selector refactor into a shared package for the future MCP surface (owner scope guard — that question belongs to M4.3b).

### 15.3 Canonical state amendment — `group` (amends §6)

- New field **`group: GroupBy`**, `type GroupBy = 'none' | 'skill'`, default `'none'`; `GROUP_VALUES = ['none', 'skill'] as const satisfies readonly GroupBy[]`; `PARAM.group = 'group'`. Value `skill` names the dimension (the skill-category taxonomy — same noun as the `skill` facet param); `category` is deliberately not used (it names the AI facet).
- **Codec:** default omitted; invalid value → `'none'` (exactly like `view`: parse returns the default, the URL is not proactively rewritten); repeated scalar → last VALID (URL-4 rule); normalize is idempotent; round-trips through normalize → serialize → parse.
- **Emit position:** directly after `skill` — canonical order becomes `view, scope, skill, group, q, sort, direction, facets, booleans, release/hydration, density, page` (§6 amended). The URL-1 full-state golden grows to 20 fields (test-only change).
- **Page reset:** `group` joins `PAGE_RESET_FIELDS` — a genuine change of `group` resets `page → 1`; a no-op write does not; an explicit `page` in the same update wins (§6.3 mechanism unchanged).
- **Clear-all (D4):** `reset()` preserves `group` alongside `view` and `density` — `group` is a presentation preference, not a filter. It is **not** a chip and never contributes to `activeFilterCount`, the `Filters N` badge, or "· filtered". Leaving grouped mode is done with the Group control (→ `None`), never by clear-all.
- **Requested vs effective (fail-soft; §6.5):** `effectiveGroup = state.group === 'skill' && skillsReady ? 'skill' : 'none'` where `skillsReady` is the existing coherent-ready conjunction (`:123`). The requested value is retained in the URL and shown by the control; it is **never** reconciled/rewritten (it becomes valid when the layer loads). Only `page` is reconciled (§6.2/§15.5).
- **`group` is presentation-only (owner amendment, D2/D3):** it MUST NOT enter `dashboardToView()`, `FilterState`, `applyFilters`, or the selector universe in any form (explicitly forbidden: `skillsScope = skillsReady && (scope === 'skills' || group === 'skill')`). `select.ts`, `filters.ts`, `sorting.ts`, `search.ts` need no change and are OUT.

### 15.4 Grouping pipeline (pure projection after selection)

```
prepare
   ↓
search / filters / explicit scope / skill facet     (unchanged; §2.2/§4.11 readiness gating unchanged)
   ↓
sort                                                 (unchanged; stable, node_id tiebreak)
   ↓
results                ← canonical effective result set — the SAME set list view shows
   ↓
group presentation     (only when effectiveGroup === 'skill')
   ├── classified   → stable partition by repo.skills.primaryCategoryId; buckets in taxonomy `order`
   └── unclassified → omitted from grouped rendering + counted (excludedCount)
```

- **Definitions:** `groupedRenderable = results.filter((r) => r.skills !== null)`; `excludedCount = results.length − groupedRenderable.length`; `groupedCount = groupedRenderable.length`; `groups = [{ id, label, repos }]` for every taxonomy category with ≥1 repo, in `categories` order, `repos` in `results` order. (`r.skills` is non-null only under a coherent-ready join, so the projection is well-defined exactly when `effectiveGroup === 'skill'`; the flat path never computes it.)
- **Bucket key = `primaryCategoryId` only** (§8). A repo appears in exactly one group; secondaries stay badges/metadata on the card.
- **Group order = taxonomy `order`** (`categories[]` as loaded — I-5): never alphabetical, never data-mined, never by count. **Empty groups are omitted** (D7): the facet already shows the full taxonomy; a zero-count heading is noise.
- **Within-group order = the effective sort restricted to the group** — the partition is stable and never re-sorts (D12-adjacent). Changing `sort`/`direction` re-orders inside every group; group order is unaffected.
- **Secondary-only facet match (D12):** `skill=[X]` admits a repo whose _secondary_ is X; in grouped mode it renders under its **primary** group, so a heading outside the filter set may legitimately appear ("filter Security, see an Mcp-integrations heading"). Correct by §8 and by "filters apply before grouping"; not a defect. Hiding such repos in grouped mode would make the grouped set ≠ the flat set and is forbidden.
- **Explicit `scope=skills`** (canonical, operable in grouped mode — D3) narrows `results` to classified repos before grouping ⇒ `excludedCount === 0`. `scope=all + group=skill` is a valid state whose omitted unclassified matches are disclosed (§15.7). Switching back to list never reveals a scope change, because none happened.
- Relies on I-4 (every primary id is a taxonomy member) — no runtime salvage bucket, no "Unclassified" pseudo-category (U1). Absence of a classification is not a classification.
- **Implementation shape (proposed):** a pure function in a new `features/repositories/group.ts`: `groupByPrimaryCategory(results: readonly SearchableRepo[], categories: readonly SkillsCategory[]) → { groups, groupedCount, excludedCount }` — no DOM, no clock, no state; memoized in the view on `[results, categories, effectiveGroup]`.

### 15.5 Pagination contract (D1)

- `effectiveGroup === 'skill'` ⇒ the **complete** `groupedRenderable` set renders on one page: no 48-item slicing, no pager, `lastPage = 1`. Definition: `lastPage = effectiveGroup === 'skill' ? 1 : max(1, ceil(results.length / PAGE_SIZE))`; `effectivePage` and the reconciliation effect are unchanged, so a requested `page > 1` converges to `1` by the existing `replace` rewrite (no history entry). Every group heading count is a whole-group count, never a page slice.
- Bookmark `?group=skill&page=3` with the layer not yet ready ⇒ flat list at page 3 (fail-soft, §15.6); once the layer is ready grouped mode becomes effective and `page` converges to 1. Leaving grouped mode afterwards starts at page 1 (`group` is a reset field; the earlier page is not restored — accepted).
- **Rejected:** grouping the current 48-slice (fragments categories across pages; heading counts become per-page and misleading). **Fallback design, not built:** ordered-slice pagination (48 over the category-ordered list, "continued" headings, whole-group counts) — only if D10 fires.
- **D10 — owner re-entry trigger, NOT a runtime mode switch:** if the unfiltered ready-state `groupedRenderable` count exceeds **400** (live today: 165; bounded by the curated taxonomy), or the browser smoke shows a clear rendering regression, the owner re-rules the pagination contract. The code never switches modes on a threshold.

### 15.6 Requested vs effective — fail-soft matrix (requested `group=skill`)

| Layer state                                                     | `effectiveGroup` | Results area                                    | Pagination            | Notice (`degraded-notice`, `role="status"`)       | Group control                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `loading`                                                       | `none`           | flat base list (§2.2 — never suppressed)        | normal 48/page        | N-L (§15.7)                                       | shows requested `Skill category`; operable    |
| `unavailable`                                                   | `none`           | flat base list                                  | normal                | N-U (§15.7)                                       | same — recoverable, URL retains `group=skill` |
| `ready`, `results.length === 0`                                 | `skill`          | existing `NoResults` (unchanged)                | —                     | none                                              | operable                                      |
| `ready`, `results.length > 0`, `groupedRenderable.length === 0` | `skill`          | grouped-empty state E-G (§15.7)                 | —                     | none                                              | operable                                      |
| `ready`, ≥1 group                                               | `skill`          | grouped sections, all `groupedRenderable` cards | single page, no pager | none — summary discloses `excludedCount` when > 0 | operable                                      |

Accepted consequences: the first paint while `loading` is flat and flips to grouped when the layer settles (the M24-FS-3 pattern; base repos never suppressed). The existing skills **filter** degraded notice (M24-FS-2/3 wording) is unchanged and independent; the group notice is a separate `<p>` so both can coexist when both `scope`/`skill` and `group` are requested. `suppressedSkillsFilterCount` / `effectiveFilterCount` are unchanged; `group` never enters them.

### 15.7 Result states & exact wording

- **Summary line (grouped, ready, ≥1 group)** — the single `<p className="result-count" role="status">`, composed exactly as (`G = groupedCount`, `M = groups.length`, `E = excludedCount`, `q = state.query.trim()`):

  ```text
  `${G} classified ${G === 1 ? 'repository' : 'repositories'} in ${M} ${M === 1 ? 'category' : 'categories'}`
    + (q ? ` for "${q}"` : '')
    + (effectiveFilterCount > 0 ? ' · filtered' : '')
    + (E > 0 ? ` · ${E} matching ${E === 1 ? 'repository' : 'repositories'} without Skills classification ${E === 1 ? 'is' : 'are'} not shown` : '')
  ```

  Live example (`scope=all`, no query, no filters): **165 classified repositories in 24 categories · 701 matching repositories without Skills classification are not shown** (the owner-pinned wording). `G` is never presented as the full search-result count. When `E > 0` a **Show as list** button (`<button type="button">`, `update({ group: 'none' })`) renders as the immediate sibling **after** the summary `<p>` — outside the `role="status"` live region, so the announced text stays the summary alone.

- **N-L (requested group, `loading`):** "Skills classification is still loading — grouped view will apply once it’s ready. Showing the list view meanwhile."
- **N-U (requested group, `unavailable`):** "Skills classification is unavailable, so grouped view isn’t applied and results are shown as a list. Group stays in your link and applies once the layer loads."
- **E-G (grouped-empty: `results.length > 0 && groupedRenderable.length === 0`):** `<div className="no-results">` with `<h3>No classified repositories in this result set</h3>`, `<p>{E} matching {repository|repositories} {has|have} no Skills classification, so grouped view has nothing to show.</p>`, a **Show as list** button (always), and a **Clear filters** button **only when `effectiveFilterCount > 0 || state.query.trim() !== ''`** (`reset()` + focus `#results-heading`; `group` preserved by D4, so the cleared state re-renders grouped over the full classified set).
- **`results.length === 0`:** the existing `NoResults` — unchanged, regardless of `group`.
- **Flat mode:** `resultSummary` unchanged.

### 15.8 UX / DOM / accessibility contract

- **Toolbar control:** `<label className="group"><span>Group</span><select value={state.group} onChange={(e) => update({ group: e.target.value as GroupBy })}>` with options `none → "None"`, `skill → "Skill category"`; placed after `Density`, same markup pattern and push history mode; always reflects the **requested** value; never disabled.
- **Skills-scope control (D3):** unchanged — independent, reflects canonical `scope`, operable in grouped mode; no implied / checked+disabled state, no delayed-effect state.
- **Grouped DOM:** inside the existing `<section className="results" aria-labelledby="results-heading">`, replacing the flat `<ul className="card-list">` + pager: `<div className="result-groups">` → per group `<section className="result-group" aria-labelledby="group-{id}">` → `<h3 id="group-{id}" className="result-group-heading">{label} <span className="result-group-count">· {n}</span></h3>` → `<ul className="card-list">` of `RepositoryCard` with **`headingLevel={4}`**. Heading hierarchy in grouped mode: `h2` results → `h3` group → `h4` card; flat mode unchanged (`h3` cards; `headingLevel` defaults to 3). `{n}` = the whole group's count. Class names are deliberately distinct from the toolbar's `.group` label (which follows the `.sort` / `.density` state-field naming) so no selector can hit both. `{id}` is the taxonomy category id — a lowercase kebab slug by contract (§4.2, `scalars.ts`), unique per artifact (I-4 domain), hence a valid and unique DOM id.
- **Density:** each per-group `.card-list` inherits the existing `.density-*` rules unchanged (compact gap/minmax); no new density behaviour.
- **Mobile / narrow:** ≤900px groups stack single-column under the existing rules; no new breakpoint; the drawer and its Skills facet are unaffected.
- **Sticky toolbar:** untouched; group headings are **not** sticky and there is **no** jump index (D9 — stacking/overlap risk against `.toolbar` / `.view-tabs` and the ≤520px static toolbar; not required by grouping).
- **Keyboard / focus:** the only new interactive elements are the Group select and the Show-as-list buttons; chip removal / clear-all keep focusing `#results-heading`. No new focus traps; no new ARIA roles beyond the labelled sections.
- **No visual redesign** beyond: group heading + count styling, `.result-groups` / `.result-group` spacing, `.toolbar .group` (same rule set as `.toolbar .density`, incl. the ≤520px full-width rule), and placement of the Show-as-list button.

### 15.9 Scope freeze

**IN:** `group` canonical field + codec / normalize / page-reset / clear-all semantics (§15.3 + the §6 amendments) · toolbar Group control · pure grouping projection (`group.ts`) · grouped rendering (taxonomy-ordered `h3` headings with whole-group counts, per-group `.card-list`, `headingLevel`) · U1 exclusion disclosure (summary + Show as list) · fail-soft matrix + N-L / N-U notices · E-G grouped-empty state with the Clear-filters condition · `lastPage = 1` in grouped mode · minimal CSS · tests + bounded evidence (§15.10–15.11) · this §15 + the §3 / §6 amendments.

**OUT:** any change to `select.ts` / `filters.ts` / `sorting.ts` / `search.ts` semantics · `scope` semantics or the Skills facet UI · an "Unclassified" group · card duplication · sticky group headings / jump index / per-group collapse · ordered-slice pagination (fallback design only) · classification, loader, artifacts, generator, `skills-staging/` · F4 / F15 / R8-B / R12-5 / the three residuals · shared-package extraction of the selectors (M4.3b) · Insights / MCP / BYOK · M3 identity · theme / layout redesign · `content-visibility`.

### 15.10 Acceptance criteria (IDs = test names; ✅ only with terminal completion markers)

| ID          | Criterion                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Mechanism                                                    |
| ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| M41-URL-1   | `group` round-trips; default omitted; invalid → `none`; repeated → last valid; canonical order `view, scope, skill, group, q, …`; the full-state golden (20 fields) is byte-exact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `dashboard-state.test.ts` (URL-1 / ORDER updated + new case) |
| M41-RST-1   | `group` change resets `page → 1`; a no-op does not; explicit `page` wins; `reset()` preserves `group` (with `view`, `density`) and clears `scope` / `skillCategories`; `popstate` restores `group`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `use-dashboard-state.test.tsx`                               |
| M41-GRP-1   | primary A + secondary B ⇒ the card renders **exactly once**, under A; B's group does not contain it (one DOM node for that `node_id`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `group.test.ts` + `grouped-view.test.tsx`                    |
| M41-ORD-1   | group order = taxonomy `order` — fixture with labels alphabetically inverted vs `order`, data insertion inverted, counts non-monotonic ⇒ headings still in `order`; empty categories omitted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `group.test.ts`                                              |
| M41-SRT-1   | within-group order = effective sort (name asc; `starred_at` desc; `node_id` tiebreak); the partition never re-sorts; changing `sort` re-orders inside groups, not across                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `group.test.ts` + `grouped-view.test.tsx`                    |
| M41-FLT-1   | filters apply before grouping: a language facet narrows groups and drops emptied ones; `skill=[X]` secondary-only match renders under its primary heading (D12)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `grouped-view.test.tsx`                                      |
| M41-SRCH-1  | search applies before grouping: a query narrows the effective result set with the existing search-enrichment semantics (unchanged — a repo may match through a secondary-category label); every matching classified repo then renders under its **primary** group, so a query may produce multiple primary groups. Fixture: Repo A `primary=security`; Repo B `primary=mcp-integrations, secondary=security`; `q=security` ⇒ both render, A under Security, B under Mcp-integrations (two groups). ### 15.11 Bounded evidence plan (D13 — no hardening loop)

- **Falsification — exactly one pass, RED-first, then reverted byte-clean.** Five mutants, each with one designated load-bearing oracle that MUST actually execute and RED for the predicted semantic reason; collateral REDs in other relevant tests are permitted and recorded — they do not invalidate the mutation proof. A mutant does not count if the mutation was not actually applied, the designated oracle did not execute, or its RED was caused by a setup/runtime failure. Mutant → designated oracle: (1) bucket by `secondaryCategoryIds[0] ?? primaryCategoryId` → M41-GRP-1; (2) order buckets alphabetically by label → M41-ORD-1; (3) re-sort inside buckets by `node_id` → M41-SRT-1; (4) drop the `skillsReady` term from `effectiveGroup` → M41-FS-1; (5) slice `results` to 48 before grouping → M41-PAG-1. No mutation arms for wording, CSS, or non-load-bearing DOM.
- **Browser smoke:** one pass, 3 states × 2 widths, terminal markers, no repo pollution.
- **Gates:** `pnpm -r typecheck` · `eslint .` · `prettier --check` · `vitest run` · Vite build — terminal completion markers recorded.
- **Review (owner-locked):** after implementation freeze, **one fresh 3-leg** (Grok + Sol + Luna) on the same frozen candidate, isolated read-only worktrees, identity-confirmed. Only a **reproduced correctness finding** triggers amend + rerun; 3/3 PROCEED + zero surviving ⇒ stop. **No reassurance rounds.**
- Then the M3 chain without the R-loop: owner commit gate → owner push/PR → exact-head CI → owner merge ruling → M4.1 closure record (evidence-only, accretive).

### 15.14 Owner rulings (2026-09-28)

- **D1 APPROVED** — complete grouped result set on one page; `page` converges to 1 when grouped mode becomes effective; `>400` is an owner re-entry trigger only, never an automatic runtime mode switch.
- **D2 APPROVED as U1 with a MUST-AMEND** — unclassified repos are omitted from the grouped **presentation**, but `group=skill` must not implicitly change `scope` or the selector universe; existing search / filter / explicit-scope / skill-facet semantics run first, sort next, grouping is a pure projection of the resulting ordered set (`groupedRenderable` / `excludedCount` as in §15.4).
- **D3 REJECTED checked+disabled** — the Skills-scope control stays independent, reflects canonical `scope`, and is operable in grouped mode; `scope=all + group=skill` is valid and disclosed; explicit `scope=skills` makes `excludedCount` zero. Consequence: `select.ts` needs no `group`-driven change.
- **D4 APPROVED** — clear-all preserves `group`, like `view` and `density`.
- **Additional** — distinguish `results.length === 0` (existing `NoResults`) from `results.length > 0 && groupedRenderable.length === 0` (E-G + Show as list; Clear filters only when filters are active); the summary pins "N classified repositories in M categories · E matching repositories without Skills classification are not shown".
- **D5–D13 ratified** (`group=skill` / default `none`; serialization after `skill`; empty groups omitted; `h2 → h3 → h4`; sticky headings / jump index OUT; D10 re-entry-only; §15 + §6 placement; secondary-only match under its primary group; bounded evidence). **Review lock:** one fresh 3-leg after implementation freeze; amend + rerun only on a reproduced correctness finding; 3/3 PROCEED + zero surviving ⇒ stop.
- **Scope guards** — no pre-emptive extraction of `select/filter/sort/search` into a shared package for MCP (M4.3b decides); `packages/insights` topology waits for M4.3a orientation. M4.2 priors (F4 / R8-B CLOSE NOW, F15 OBSOLETE) stay hypotheses for M4.2's executable adjudication; at most 1–2 coherent items there.

**Implementation decisions taken inside the contract (flagged for the owner; none reopens a ruling):**

1. **Summary-line scoping.** §15.7 pins the grouped composition for "grouped, ready, ≥1 group" and says nothing about the summary in E-G. Implemented literally: the grouped `groupedResultSummary` renders only when `grouped && grouped.groups.length > 0`; **E-G and the zero-result state keep the flat `resultSummary` of the actual result set** (e.g. `4 results for "apple/"`, `0 results for "zzz"`). Rationale: the exclusion is already explained once inside E-G (`{E} matching repositories have no Skills classification…`) — a "0 classified repositories in 0 categories · 4 … not shown" summary would say it twice and announce a contradiction-shaped sentence through the live region. Pinned by M41-FS-3 / M41-FS-4.
2. **Post-summary Show-as-list condition.** The button after the summary `<p>` renders only when `groups.length > 0 && excludedCount > 0`; in E-G the single Show-as-list button is the one inside the E-G block (§15.7 "always"). M41-FS-3 asserts exactly one Show-as-list button in E-G.
3. **`groupedCount`** counts every result whose skills record is present (relies on I-4: a classified repo's primary is always a taxonomy id), so `excludedCount = results.length − groupedCount` — unit-pinned (`group.test.ts`).
4. **`grouped` memo requires `skillCategories`** (TS narrowing; the categories are always defined once `skillsReady`), so the projection is `null` in every non-ready state and the fail-soft matrix (§15.6) is decided by `effectiveGroup` alone.

### 16.1 Owner adjudication — round-1 ruling (FROZEN; the immutable M4.2 contract)

Seven dispositions, owner-ruled 2026-09-28 on the orientation record (`m42-orientation/ORIENTATION-R1.md`; executable evidence = 19 temporary probes [8 + 3 + 8], 210/210 AI-pair history sweep through `verifyAiArtifacts`, 90/90 residual pins). Priors were NOT carried forward as conclusions; each item was re-answered by executable evidence.

1. **F4 — AI readiness-coherence parity (§4.11) = CLOSE NOW / IMPLEMENT (slot 1).** Fix the whole coherent-readiness defect family as ONE coherent fix (not two debt items): (a) in `RepositoryView`, AI is effective-ready only when `annotationStatus === 'ready'` AND annotations data is non-null; (b) `dashboardToView`'s omitted `aiReady` default flips from fail-open `true` to fail-closed `false`, symmetric with `skillsReady`. Requested AI URL/filter state is preserved under degraded conditions; ONLY effective filtering is neutralized. Real App coherent-ready behavior is unchanged. Orientation P1/P2/P3 evidence becomes narrow permanent regression coverage (coherent ready · ready-without-data · omitted-readiness/data-only · real App wiring + M0 fail-soft preservation). **Do not broaden AI semantics.**
2. **F15 — classification mount-fetch sequencing (§4.11/§4.12) = CLOSED BY RATIFICATION, record-only.** Behavior still reproduces (NOT labelled obsolete). Executable evidence (F15-P1–P3) establishes no correctness consequence and confirms the previously owner-accepted mount-anchored lifecycle. Do not change fetch sequencing; add no permanent lifecycle pins. Consumes no implementation slot.
3. **R8-B — discovery `node_id` uniqueness / `source_count` parity (§4.10) = KEEP DEFERRED WITH TRIGGER.** No implementation in M4.2. Reopen and close it before either (a) the first discovery artifact pair is merged to `main`, or (b) any MCP/API surface exposes discovery artifacts. Do NOT freeze a normative `source_count` formula now.
4. **R12-5 — AI canonical-form parity (§4.10 Ruling 2) = KEEP DEFERRED WITH TRIGGER.** Current guarantees remain exact-byte integrity + pair consistency, with canonical serialized form enforced through the current publication verification path rather than browser runtime. Reopen if that publication path is bypassed or a consumer requires canonical serialized form.
   5–7. **Three accepted residuals (§4.10 Ruling-1 last-write-wins pair concurrency · §4.10 R15 crash-between-renames torn pair · §4.12 S2 pair-local stuck lock) = KEEP, unchanged.** No new executable evidence invalidated their dispositions; not reopened.

### 16.2 F4 scope & implementation (the ONLY slot)

Production delta = **two lines, one coherent fix** (`apps/dashboard/src/features/repositories/`):

- `RepositoryView.tsx`: `const aiReady = annotationStatus === 'ready' && annotations != null;` (was `annotationStatus ? annotationStatus === 'ready' : annotations != null`) — now identical in shape to the skills gate (`skillsStatus === 'ready' && skillsClassification != null`). Closes both fail-open mirrors: ready + null (F4-P1, the silent match-nothing zeroing) and omitted-status + data (F4-P2, the former M0 data-presence fallback).
- `select.ts`: `dashboardToView(s, aiReady = false, skillsReady = false)` (was `aiReady = true`) — fail-closed default, symmetric with `skillsReady`.

**Scope boundary (owner "do not broaden AI semantics").** The AI data JOIN (`annotationsByNodeId = annotations?.byNodeId`, feeding badges / the enriched count / facet options) is deliberately NOT gated by `aiReady` — it stays driven by data presence, exactly as before. In production the two are equivalent (App sets `annotations` non-null iff status `ready`); they diverge only in the App-unreachable incoherent shapes the fix now neutralizes AT THE FILTER, per the ruling ("only effective filtering is neutralized"). The sole production caller (`RepositoryView`) passes `aiReady` explicitly, so the default flip changes NO production path.

**Comment coherence (traces to the fix, not creep).** The now-false narratives the change created were corrected in place: `RepositoryView.tsx` "deliberately STRICTER than the AI layer's data-presence fallback" → "symmetric … F4 closed the AI layer's former M0 fallback", and its `annotationStatus` prop JSDoc "Defaults from `annotations`" → "Activation requires `'ready'` AND data … omitted ⇒ not ready" (this last one was missed in the initial sweep and caught by the pre-commit review — the 3/3-converged round-1 finding, §16.7); `select.ts` JSDoc "Defaults to `true`" → "`false` (fail-closed) since F4", and skills' "Unlike `aiReady`" → "Like `aiReady` (since F4)".

### 16.3 Narrow permanent regression coverage (P1/P2/P3 → pins)

New file `apps/dashboard/src/features/ai-readiness-coherence.test.tsx` (5 pins) + 1 pin in `select.test.ts` — the four owner-named cases plus the default:

- **M42-F4-COHERENT** — ready + data ⇒ the requested AI filter activates and is counted ("1 of 2 · filtered", "Filters 1", no notice).
- **M42-F4-READY-NO-DATA** (F4-P1) — ready + null ⇒ NOT ready: results never zeroed ("2 of 2 repositories"), filter not counted, degraded notice shown, `?category=security` retained in the URL.
- **M42-F4-DATA-ONLY** (F4-P2) — omitted status + data ⇒ NOT ready: data presence alone never activates; base set preserved, URL retained.
- **M42-F4-APP-NULL / M42-F4-APP-DATA** (F4-P3) — real App wiring: `annotationsLoader → null` ⇒ App sets (unavailable, null), base browser intact + notice, never zeroed; `→ data` ⇒ (ready, data), filter active, notice cleared.
- **M42-F4-DEFAULT** (`select.test.ts`) — `dashboardToView(state)` with `aiReady` omitted is fail-closed (categories/aiTags `[]`); symmetric with skills M24-STS-3.

**Test churn** (existing tests written to the OLD fail-open contract, moved to EXPLICIT readiness — intent-preserving, authorized by the ruling as a contract change): `RepositoryView.test.tsx` R3-ready + INT-2 (add explicit `annotationStatus: 'ready'` alongside the annotations App would set together); `select.test.ts` M0-FS-1 (explicit `dashboardToView(state, true)` for the ready arm). No other test relied on the fallback — verified by enumerating every annotations render site; UI-7's enriched-count path is data-driven, ungated, and stays green unchanged.

Each arm confirmed the pins DISCRIMINATE (can go RED), then was reverted byte-clean (backup sha256 re-matched):

- **Arm 1 — remove the DATA half** (`aiReady = annotationStatus === 'ready'`): designated oracle **M42-F4-READY-NO-DATA** RED for the predicted reason (fail-open reopened ⇒ ready + null reactivates the filter ⇒ DOM shows "0 of 2 · filtered" where "2 of 2 repositories" was asserted); the other 4 pins in the file stayed GREEN ⇒ discriminates the DATA half specifically.
- **Arm 2 — restore the fail-open DEFAULT** (`aiReady = true`): designated oracle **M42-F4-DEFAULT** RED for the predicted reason (`dashboardToView(state)` returns `['security']` where `[]` was asserted); the other 8 pins in the file stayed GREEN (M0-FS-1 uses explicit `true`) ⇒ discriminates the DEFAULT half specifically.

## 17. M4.3a — Deterministic Insights: charter, contract & acceptance boundary (CONTRACT FROZEN 2026-09-30 · IMPLEMENTED + LOCAL GATES + BROWSER SMOKE 2026-09-30 · PRE-COMMIT REVIEW ROUND 1 = 6 findings FIXED · ROUND 2 = 4 · ROUND 3 = 4 · ROUND 4 = 1 · ROUND 5 = 3/3 literal PROCEED, PRE-COMMIT REVIEW CLOSED 2026-09-30 at §17.6 · M4.3a CLOSED 2026-09-30 — merged #304 as `a49adb03`, terminal closure §17.7)

Kickoff 2026-09-30 (owner direction, §3 programme: M4.3a deterministic Insights → M4.3b read-only MCP → BYOK only after (b)). M4.3a adds a third top-level view — a read-only **Insights** surface of deterministic summaries over the SAME loaded stars dataset. The contract was frozen by the owner from the round-1 orientation (recorded outside the tree in `m43-orientation/ORIENTATION-R1.md`, read-only); this §17 is the implementation charter. Written on `feat/m4.3a-deterministic-insights`.

### 17.0 Freeze anchors (immutable — do not re-litigate)

- `M4_2_CLOSURE_SHA = 54bd5174` (PR #301, §16.8) · `M4_2_DOC_CLOSURE_SHA = 626dbc5` (PR #302).
- **`M4_3A_BASE_SHA = 026b93fa327c42afc00cec685f69f2630ec1e122`** — fresh `origin/main` at kickoff. The owner ruling cited `626dbc5`; between the ruling and the branch cut the daily sync + an AI-artifact PR (#303) advanced `origin/main` `626dbc5 → 026b93fa` by **data-only** commits (`stars.json`, `ai-annotations*.json`, `dataset-meta.json`; **zero** change under `apps/`, `packages/`, `docs/` — the code and this spec are byte-identical to `626dbc5`). Branched off fresh `origin/main@026b93fa` per standing practice ("branch off freshly-fetched origin/main"); the data drift does not affect the code or the fixture-based tests, and is disclosed here as a deliberate base-update.
- **Baseline gate:** full `vitest run` on `026b93fa` = **1309/1309** (117 files); dashboard-scoped = 324 (26 files).
- **`skills-staging/`** (14 untracked files, fp `69830a75…`) stays byte-for-byte untouched. M2 / M3 / M4.1 / M4.2 are CLOSED and not reopened; F15/R8-B/R12-5 + the three residuals stay independent open debt, not touched here.
- **Programme guardrails (owner):** deterministic-only; no longitudinal/trend claim the stored data cannot support; dashboard-local pure functions, NO package extraction; per-card optional-layer degradation; repo browse state preserved-but-inactive; no LG / CD / RC / git-history reconstruction / MCP / BYOK; commit / push / PR / merge remain owner-gated.

### 17.1 Frozen contract (from ORIENTATION-R1, owner-frozen)

- **CRUX (binds all wording):** the runtime data is a SINGLE current snapshot (`stars.json` + per-repo `starred_at`/`pushed_at`/…); there is NO longitudinal collection-composition series. So a per-period count is "repos currently held, by when they were starred" (unstarred repos are absent entirely) — NEVER a trend, growth, or "interests shifting". No insight may imply category-popularity-over-time.
- **Scope = EXACTLY four insights:** **PC** primary-category distribution (AI layer) · **SA** starring activity by UTC month (canonical) · **ST** forgotten/stale stars (canonical, reusing `DerivedRepo.isStale` @ `STALE_MONTHS=12` exactly) · **CC** classification coverage (skills layer, curated-subset + provenance-staleness). The optional LG (language distribution), CD (category-by-starred-date), RC (similar clusters), and any git-history reconstruction are OUT.
- **Provenance contract:** every insight carries a `Provenance` (method · sourceFields · formula) and a `Coverage` (n · of · layer · generation provenance for a generated layer), rendered beside it — no opaque score without an explicit deterministic formula.
- **Per-card fail-soft:** each card degrades INDEPENDENTLY — PC needs the AI layer coherent-ready and CC needs the skills layer coherent-ready (the F4/§4.11 status-AND-data gate); SA and ST are canonical and always render.

### 17.2 §6 amendment (minimal — the only canonical-state change)

`view` gains a third value `insights` (§6.4, amended): a deterministic read-only view of the same dataset, **always available** once stars load (it never falls back like `discovery`), so the view-tabs nav is now **always present** (`Starred` + `Insights`, plus `Discovery Inbox` when available). While `insights` is active the repo browse state (query / filters / sort / group / scope) is **preserved in the URL but INACTIVE** — RepositoryView is not mounted — re-applying on return to `stars`; `page` follows the existing §6.3 view-switch reset. NO new facet/filter/canonical field; the codec accepts `view=insights` through the existing `VIEW_VALUES`/`asView` path (round-trips; unknown → default `stars`).

### 17.3 Implementation (dashboard-local, pure)

- `features/insights/insights.ts` — four pure functions over `DerivedRepo[]` (`deriveAll`, reused): `computeCategoryDistribution` (group by `ai.category`, sort count-desc then name-asc) · `computeStarringActivity` (UTC-month bucket of `starred_at`, contiguous zero-fill, window) · `computeStaleStars` (reuse `isStale`; unknown push reported apart, never stale) · `computeClassificationCoverage` (live join `matched`; stale = generated-against-sha ≠ live sha; unresolved from meta). No DOM, no clock, no state.
- `features/insights/InsightsView.tsx` — the four cards; coherent-ready gates (`annotationStatus === 'ready' && annotations != null`; skills likewise) join the layer maps only when ready; distributions render as accessible `<table>`s (the table IS the text equivalent; the bar is an `aria-hidden` decorative width cue); a provenance footer on every card.
- `app/App.tsx` — `view=insights` in `effectiveView`; the view-tabs nav always present (§6.4); the page-reconciliation effect parks (does not canonicalize) the browse state in insights. `state/dashboard-state.ts` — `DashboardView` + `VIEW_VALUES` gain `insights`. `styles.css` — insight-card grid (2-col desktop, 1-col ≤ the auto-fill breakpoint), accessible table + decorative bar.

### 17.4 Evidence boundary (owner-specified; each satisfied)

- **Deterministic formula/unit tests** — `insights.test.ts` (17): PC grouping/sort/coverage + `generatedAt`; SA UTC-month bucketing, **zero-fill** (incl. year boundary), **UTC month boundary**, and **STRICT validation** (invalid month `2026-13`, normalized-impossible `2026-02-30`, extended year `10000-01`, and timezone-less/offset non-`Z` instants all EXCLUDED ⇒ deterministic + guaranteed-terminating zero-fill); ST reuse of `isStale` + threshold + unknown-push; CC live join + stale flag + unresolved + null-layer.
- **URL round-trip** — `dashboard-state.test.ts` M43-VIEW-INSIGHTS (`view=insights` round-trips; unknown → `stars`).
- **Browse-state preserved-but-inactive** — `App.test.tsx` INSIGHTS-2 (filters + query parked in the URL while insights renders and RepositoryView is unmounted; re-applied active on return to Starred). INSIGHTS-1 pins the always-present tabs.
- **AI/Skills card-level degradation** — `InsightsView.test.tsx` IV-3/IV-4 (PC unavailable/loading), IV-8 (CC unavailable), coherent-ready gate.
- **SA UTC boundary + zero-fill + wording** — IV-5 (buckets incl. zero-filled gap; "not a trend" text) + the SA unit tests.
- **ST exact reuse** — IV-6 + ST-1/ST-2 (`isStale`/`STALE_MONTHS` cross-checked against `deriveAll`).
- **CC curated-subset / provenance wording** — IV-7 ("curated subset", "not classification failures", "earlier snapshot").
- **A11y equivalent for any visual chart** — distributions ARE `<table>`s (rowheader + count cell); the bar is `aria-hidden` decoration.
- **Desktop + narrow/mobile browser smoke** — real production build served with the live 868-repo dataset; `?view=insights` at 1280px (2-col) and 375px (1-col). All four cards rendered correct real numbers (PC developer-tools 228 / ai-ml 205 …; SA 2025-04 → 2026-09 zero-filled, peak 2026-07 = 169; ST 26/868 stale; CC 165/868 curated-subset, generated 2026-08-13 against an earlier snapshot, 5 unresolved), the always-present `Starred` + `Insights` tabs, and NO console error except the expected pre-existing discovery-`404` fail-soft (discovery not published). Local gates (final): full `vitest run` **1340/1340** (119 files), `pnpm -r typecheck` clean, `eslint`/`prettier` clean on the candidate.

### 17.5 Execution order & the stop

`fetch` → anchors (§17.0) → base-drift disclosed (data-only) → branch cut ✅ → §6 amendment (§17.2) ✅ → pure functions + InsightsView + App wiring + styles (§17.3) ✅ → the evidence boundary + browser smoke (§17.4) ✅ → **NEXT: one fresh isolated 3-leg pre-commit review (Grok + Sol + Luna) over the whole candidate; 3/3 literal PROCEED + zero surviving in-boundary finding ⇒ M4.3a PRE-COMMIT REVIEW CLOSED** → owner commit gate → owner push / PR → exact-head CI → owner merge ruling → M4.3a closure record. **No commit / push / PR** until the owner commit gate; `skills-staging/` untouched. **Review object = 10 paths** (9 code + this spec): `app/App.tsx` · `app/App.test.tsx` · `state/dashboard-state.ts` · `state/dashboard-state.test.ts` · `styles.css` · `features/insights/insights.ts` (new) · `features/insights/insights.test.ts` (new) · `features/insights/InsightsView.tsx` (new) · `features/insights/InsightsView.test.tsx` (new) + this spec (§17 + §6.4).

### 17.6 Pre-commit review — round 1 (fresh isolated 3-leg) → six findings fixed → round 2

**Round 1** (2026-09-30): one fresh isolated read-only 3-leg over the frozen candidate (fingerprint `c6403f74…`), packet byte-identical across legs (`26750627…`); Sol/Luna got detached worktrees at `026b93fa` (fingerprint reproduced), Grok packet-only; verdicts written outside every leg. Dry-run pings confirmed served identity (Grok `modelUsage.grok-4.7-build`; codex headers `gpt-5.6-sol` / `gpt-5.6-luna`).

| Leg                | Served identity                               | Round-1 verdict                                                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Grok-4.7 @ high    | `modelUsage.grok-4.7-build`                   | **FIX** (1) — the empty-stars finding below; explicitly confirmed everything else holds |
| gpt-5.6-sol @ max  | codex header `gpt-5.6-sol · read-only · max`  | **FIX** (5) — the five findings below                                                   |
| gpt-5.6-luna @ max | codex header `gpt-5.6-luna · read-only · max` | **FIX** (base-ref) — fingerprint + 10-path status confirmed matching                    |

**Findings (all reproduced first-hand; fixed; none is a contract change):**

1. **Empty-stars early return preempts Insights** (Grok + Sol, converged). `App.tsx`'s `if (starsEmpty && !discoveryAvailable) return <EmptyState/>` fired before view resolution, so `view=insights` with zero stars and no discovery dead-ended on `EmptyState` — violating §6.4/§17.2 "always available / tabs always present". FIX: the early return now excludes `effectiveView === 'insights'`; Insights renders its own zero states. Pinned by `App.test.tsx` INSIGHTS-3 (default-stars `EMPTY-1` behavior preserved).
2. **SA accepted an invalid UTC month → potential infinite zero-fill** (Sol — CRITICAL; Grok tested only valid months and missed it). The shape-only regex admitted `2026-13`/`2026-00`, a month key `nextMonth` from `2026-12` can never reach, so the contiguous zero-fill could loop forever. FIX: `computeStarringActivity` now parses `new Date(at)`, excludes an invalid instant, and derives the UTC month from `getUTCFullYear()/getUTCMonth()` (always 01-12 ⇒ termination). Pinned by `insights.test.ts` SA-5 (the test itself would hang on regression).
3. **Provenance `formula` was defined but never rendered** (Sol). The card footer showed method + sourceFields + coverage but not the explicit `formula` the §17.1 contract names. FIX: the footer now renders `Formula: …`; pinned by IV-9 (`Formula:` ×4).
4. **PC coverage omitted the AI generation timestamp** (Sol). A generated layer must carry its generation provenance; PC's `ai`-layer coverage had none. FIX: `computeCategoryDistribution` takes + carries `generatedAt`, rendered as `generated <date>` in the footer; pinned by `insights.test.ts` PC-4 + IV-7.
5. **CC misdescribed generation-time `unresolved` as a live fact** (Sol). "not matched to a current repository" implied a live recomputation; `unresolved` is generation-time metadata. FIX: reworded to "unresolved at generation time (never matched to a repository when the classification was generated)".
6. **Luna base-ref** — Luna confirmed the fingerprint + 10-path status match but flagged the packet's "main == 026b93fa" against the leg's local `main` (still `626dbc5`, un-fast-forwarded). NOT a candidate defect (the detached worktree HEAD is `026b93fa` and the fingerprint matched). RESOLVED: local `main` fast-forwarded to `origin/main == 026b93fa`; the round-2 packet states the base as the worktree HEAD, not a claim about the local branch.

**After the fixes:** full `vitest run` **1336/1336** (119 files; +3 vs round 1 = SA-5, PC-4, INSIGHTS-3); typecheck / eslint / prettier clean; a **re-run desktop browser smoke** on the rebuilt bundle confirmed the corrected footers render (`Formula: …`, `generated <date>`) and the reworded CC copy, with no layout regression and only the expected discovery-`404` fail-soft. **Round 2** (2026-09-30): re-review of the amended candidate. Grok-4.7@high = **PROCEED** (all round-1 fixes hold, no new in-boundary defect; three non-blocking notes that overlapped Sol/Luna's findings). gpt-5.6-sol@max = **FIX** (2) and gpt-5.6-luna@max = **FIX** (2) — both converged on DEEPER versions of round-1's two edge areas (empty-stars routing and SA timestamp validation): the round-1 fixes were incomplete, NOT new areas — converging, not thrashing. Findings (reproduced first-hand; each fixed by REPLACING the mechanism, §5's three-defects-one-mechanism): **(7) empty-stars nav** — the full-screen `EmptyState` still preempted the always-present nav for the default `stars` view at zero stars, leaving Insights unreachable by click (round 1 fixed only URL access). FIX: the full-screen empty return is removed ENTIRELY — the view-tabs nav is always present once stars load and `EmptyState` renders IN-PANE (§6.4); EMPTY-1 strengthened. **(8) SA strict validation** — `new Date` SILENTLY NORMALIZES impossible dates (`2026-02-30` → Mar 2, misbucketed) and accepts extended/non-4-digit years whose month key `nextMonth`'s 4-digit slice can never reach (still non-terminating); the schema only guarantees a nonempty string. FIX: `computeStarringActivity` now requires a canonical 4-digit-year instant (`/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})T/`) AND a UTC round-trip (rejects `2026-02-30` and `10000-01`); SA-6 / SA-7. **(Luna page-parking)** — a bookmarked `?view=insights&page=5` with zero stars still canonicalized `page → 1`. After the reset, re-dispatched: sol = **FIX** (3), luna = **FIX** (1) — all four were EXACTLY Grok's four non-blocking notes, elevated to findings (a reviewer threshold difference, not new discovery). All reproduced + fixed: **(9) SA timezone-determinism** — the regex accepted a timezone-LESS instant, which `new Date` parses in the runtime's LOCAL zone, making the UTC bucket (and the round-trip) machine-dependent. FIX: the regex now requires a `Z` UTC designator (`…T\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}(?:\.\d+)?Z$`); SA-8. **(10) §6.4 stale sentence** — it still described the removed full-screen empty-state early return. **Scope of this record.** Evidence-only backfill of what happened after §17.6's pre-commit closure, in the §14.28 / §15.17 / §16.8 convention: the dated point-in-time records above — §17.6's five-round arc and §17.5's "NEXT" / "the stop" language — are **retained verbatim**, each accurate as written on its date and **superseded by this terminal closure**.
